5.   “	pāytiri paṭōvāp parappunīrp paṉikkaṭal!
 tūvat tuṟantaṉaṉ tuṟivaṉeṉ ṟavaṉṟiṟam
 nōyteṟa vuḻippārka ṇimiḻtiyō vempōlak
 kātalcey takaṉṟāri yuṭiyiyō nī!
 maṉṟirum peṇṇi maṭalcēr aṉṟil!
 naṉṟaṟi koṉṟaṉar avareṉak kalaṅkiya
  eṉṟuya raṟintaṉi naraṟiyō vempōla
 iṉṟuṇip pirintāri yuṭiyiyō nī.”	(kali - 129)
itaṉuḷ, kaṭalum aṉṟilum uṟṟa piṇiyit talivi tampiṇikku varuntiṉavākac cērtti, uyartiṇiyākki, uvama vāyiṟ paṭuttavāṟu kāṇka. piṟavu maṉṉa.
‘kāmaṅkaṇṇiya’ eṉṟataṉāl, kikkiḷi peruntiṇikkuṅ koḷka.	(46)
3. kaṉavil nikaḻtal
92.	“kaṉavu murittā lavviṭat tāṉa.”
i-ḷ: avviṭattāṉa - mēṟkūṟiyavi, kaṉavum urittāl - iruvarkkum kaṉaviṉ kaṇṇum nikaḻum eṉṟavāṟu.
muṟkūṟiya pālkeḻukiḷavi, talivaṉ taliviyar kaṉavilum nikaḻumeṉpatām.
kāṭṭu:
“alantāṅ kamiyalēṉ eṉṟāṉip paṟṟiyeṉ
 nalantārā yōveṉat toṭuppēṉ pōlavum,
 kalantāṅ kēyeṉ kaviṉpeṟa muyaṅkip
 pulampal ōmpeṉa aḷippāṉ pōlavum.”	(kali - 128)
itaṉuḷ, ‘paṟṟi, muyaṅki’ eṉṟatu talivaṉ uruviṉi. itu kaṉavu nili. kaṉavil kaṇṭa talivaṉ uruvamum uṟuppum uṇarvum maṟutturippatum uṭiyavākac ceyyā marapiṉa ceyvatākak kūṟiyavāṟum, avi uyartiṇiyākak kūṟiyavāṟum aṟika.	(47)


4. cevilik kaṉavu
93.	tāykku murittāṟ pōkkuṭaṉ kiḷappiṉ.
i - ḷ: talimakaḷ uṭaṉpōya kālattuc ceviliyum ivvāṟu kaṉavu kāṇpāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
pālkeḻukiḷavi ivaṭkurittātal aṭutta nūṟpāviṟ kūṟukiṟār.
talivi uṭaṉ pōkāmaṟ kāttaṟkuriya ḷātalāṉum, taliviyi eṉṟum piriyāta paḻakkattāṉum cevilikkuk kaṉavu urittāyiṟṟu.
“kaṇ paṭi peṟēeṉ kaṉava.” (akam - 55) 
eṉak kāṇka.	(48)
5. pālkeḻu kiḷavi kūṟuvōr
94.	pālkeḻu kiḷavi nālvarkku murittāl.
i - ḷ: ilakkaṇac col (cū - 46) - tōḻi, cevili, naṟṟāy, pāṅkaṉ eṉṉum nālvarkkum uritteṉṟavāṟu. ‘iruvarkku muriyapāṟ kiḷavi’ (cū - 46) eṉat talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum āṇṭē kūṟaliṉ, īṇṭu innālvarumēyām. talivaṉ taliviyarit  tāmākavē koḷvōr ivarē.
kāṭṭu:
       “	tarumaṇaṟ kiṭanta pāviyeṉ
marumaka ḷēyeṉa muyaṅkiṉa ḷaḻumē.”	            (akam - 165)
itu, naṟṟāy maṇaṟpāviyip peṇpālāk kūṟit taḻīik koṇṭeḻutaliṟ pāl keḻukiḷavi yāyiṟṟu, itu, cevilikkum poruntum. aṭutta cūttirattil ēṉiyiru variyum vilakkupa. 	(49)
6. mēlataṟkōr ciṟappu viti
95.	naṭpi ṉaṭakki yāṅkalaṅ kaṭiyē.
i - ḷ: āṅku - muṉṉi nūṟpāvil (49) kūṟiya annālvarum avvāṟu kūṟutal, naṭpiṉ naṭakki alaṅkaṭi - talivaṉum taliviyum naṭpāka naṭavāta pōtu āmeṉṟavāṟu.
naṭpiṉ naṭakki - kaḷavoḻukkam oḻukutal. alaṅkaṭi - kaḷavoḻukkatti viṭṭu uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōtal. uṭaṉ pōṉapiṉ innālvarkkum pālkeḻu kiḷavi naṭakku meṉpatām.
tōḻiyum uṭaṉpaṭṭut taliviyi aṉupputaliṉ, tōḻi kūṟāḷ. pāṅkaṉ aṟiyavē uṭaṉ pōkku nikaḻtaliṉ pāṅkaṉum kūṟāṉ. orukāl pāṅkaṉaṟiyāmal uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻiṉ, pāṅkaṉ kūṟuvāṉ. itu aruki nikaḻum.	(50)
7. taliviyik kāppōr
96.	uyiru nāṇum maṭaṉu meṉṟivi
ceyirtīr ciṟappiṉ nālvarkku muriya.
itu, talivaṉāl talivikku vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟiya vaḻi, ataṉik kāttaṟkuriyōr ivareṉkiṉṟatu.
i - ḷ: uyirum nāṇum maṭaṉum eṉṟu ivi - taliviyiṉ uyirum nāṇum maṭaṉum avaḷi viṭṭu nīṅkāmaṟ kāttal, ceyirtīr ciṟappiṉ nālvarkkum uriya - kuṟṟan tīrnta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya naṟṟāy, cevili, tōḻi, talivaṉ ākiya nālvarkkum uriyavākum eṉṟavāṟu.
talivaṉ pirivāl talivi uyir vāṭi iṟantu paṭāmalum, nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅki uṭaṉ pōkāmalum, kaṟpil nāṇ maṭaṉ nīṅki veḷippaṭak kūṟāmalum (itu peruntiṇi) tōḻi āṟṟuvittuk kākkum. talivaṉ pirivu nīṭṭiyātu kāttal. kaḷavil tōḻi cevilik kaṟattoṭu niṉṟum, cevili naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟum, naṟṟāy tanti taṉṉik kaṟattoṭu niṉṟum kāttal.
talivaṉ kaḷavil variviṭi vittup pirintum, kaṟpil parattiyiṟ pirintum ivaṟṟik kāvāṉākiyāl, talivaṉi yoḻinta mūvarkkumē perumpāṉmi uriyaveṉpatām. ummi - eccam.
pāṅkaṉ, pirivu nīṭṭiyātu kaḷaviṉum kaṟpiṉum talivaṉik kūṭṭuvittuk kāppāṉākalāṉ, talivaṉi yoḻittup pāṅkaṉoṭu nālvar eṉiṉumām.	(51)
8. talivi uṟuppiṉi uṇarntapōlak kūṟal
97.	 vaṇṇam pacantu pulampuṟu kāli
uṇarnta pōla uṟuppiṉik kiḻavi
puṇarnta vakiyiṟ puṇarkkavum peṟumē.
i - ḷ: vaṇṇam pacantu pulampuṟu kāli - pirivāl talivikku niṟam vēṟupaṭṭu mikunta tuṉpa muṟumpōtu, kiḻavi uṟuppiṉi uṇarnta pōla - talivi taṉatu uṟuppukkaḷi aṟivuṭiyaṉa pōla, puṇarnta vakiyāl puṇarkkavum peṟum - poruntiṉa kūṟṟāl viḷittuk kūṟavum peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
vaṇṇam  pacattal - uṭal pacuminiṟa mātal. itu kātal nōyāl ēṟpaṭum niṟavēṟupāṭu. pulampuṟutal - taṉippaṭar uṟutal; atāvatu, talivaṉ piriyat taṉittirukka āṟṟātu tuṉpamikutal. pulampu - taṉimi. paṭar - tuṉpam.
‘puṇarnta vaki’ eṉṟataṉāl, kātum ōtiyum mutalāyiṉa kūṟappeṟā. kaṇṇum tōḷum mārpum kūṟappeṟum. ivaṟṟit talivaṉpāṟ celavu varavuṭiyaṉa pōlak kūṟuvāḷ.
kāṭṭu:
1.   “	taṇantanāḷ cāla aṟivippa pōlum
maṇantanāḷ vīṅkiya tōḷ.”	
(kuṟaḷ)
itu, tōḷiṉi aṟivuṭiyatu pōl kūṟiyatu.
2.   “	kaṇṇuṅ koḷaccēṟi neñcē yiviyeṉṉit
tiṉṉu mavarkkāṇa luṟṟu.”		(kuṟaḷ)
itu, kaṇṇiṉit talivaṉiṭam celvaṉavākak kūṟiyatu. 		(52)
9. talivaṉic cērāmi
98.	uṭampum uyirum vāṭiyak kālum
eṉṉuṟ ṟaṉakol iviyeṉi ṉallatu
kiḻavōṟ cērtal kiḻattik killi.
i-ḷ: uṭampum uyirum vāṭiyak kālum - kūṭṭa miṉmiyāl uṭampum uyirum tēyvuṟṟa kālattum, ivi eṉ uṟṟaṉa kol eṉiṉ allatu - ‘ivi (uṭampum uyirum) eṉṉa varutta muṟṟaṉa kol’ eṉṟu taṉakku varutta millatu pōlak kūṟutalallatu, kiḻavōṉ cērtal kiḻattikku illi - talivi tāṉē ceṉṟu talivaṉi aṭiyāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
kaḷavu kaṟpu iraṇṭiṉum cērāḷeṉka. kātal mikavum kaṇavaṉic cērātu vañcam pōṉṟoḻukal vaḻuvāyiṉum amikka veṉpatām.
kāṭṭu:
      “	katumeṉat tānōkkit tāmē kaluḻum
 itunakat takka tuṭittu.”			      (kuṟaḷ)
itaṉuḷ, aṉṟu tāmē nōkki, iṉṟu tāmē irun taḻukiṉṟaṉa. itu nammāl cirikkattakka iyalpiṉi yuṭittu eṉat talivi, ikkaṇkaḷukku eṉṉa varuttamuṟṟavō eṉak kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. aṉṟu - iyaṟkip puṇarcci naṭanta pōtu.(53)
10. talivi neñcoṭucāvutal
99.	oruciṟi neñcamō ṭucāvuṅ kāli
uriya tākalum uṇṭeṉa moḻipa.
i - ḷ: oru ciṟi - taṉatu neñcatti vēṟāka vittu, neñcamoṭu ucāvuṅ kāli - atik kūṭṭattiṟku viṉāvuṅ kālam, uriya tākalum uṇṭu eṉa moḻipa - urittākalum uṇṭeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
oru ciṟi - oru pakkam. atāvatu, taṉatu neñcattit taṉṉiṉ vēṟākak koṇṭu viṉāvutalām. ummiyāl, tōḻiyoṭu ucāvutaluṅ koḷka. ucāvutal - viṉāvutal.
itu, talivaṉoṭu kūṭāmiyāl avaṉ āṟṟāṉāvaṉ eṉṟēṉum, uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭaṟkaṇ avaṉ pulakku meṉṟēṉum (kaṟ - 9) talivi ucāvutalām. uṇarppu vayiṉ vārā ūṭal - talivaṉ uṇartta uṇarātu talivi ūṭal.
kāṭṭu:
1.   “	māṇa maṟantuḷḷā nāṇilik kippōr
puṟañcāyntu kaṇṭippāy neñcē! - uṟaḻntivaṉip
poyppa viṭēe meṉaneruṅkiṟ ṟappiṉēṉ
eṉṉaṭi cērtalu  muṇṭu.”	(kali - 89)
itaṉuḷ, ‘neñcē, nāṇamillāmavaṉukku ivvūṭa pōril tōṟṟu, ataṉāluḷḷa payaṉik kāṇpāy’ eṉa neñcit taṉṉiṉ vēṟāka niṟutti viṉāyt talivi ūṭal tīrntami kāṇka.
2.   “	pakalē palaruṅ kāṇa nāṇviṭ
ṭakalvayiṟ paṭappi avaṉūr viṉavic
ceṉmō vāḻi tōḻi!”	(naṟ - 365)
itu, tōḻiyoṭu ucāviyatu.	(54)
11. talivi maṭaṉaḻital
100. 	taṉvayiṟ karattalum avaṉvayiṉ vēṭṭalum
aṉṉa viṭaṅkaḷalvaḻi yellām
maṭaṉoṭu niṟṟal kaṭaṉeṉa moḻipa.
i - ḷ: taṉ vayiṉ karattalum - talivaṉ taliviyiṭattē puṟattoḻukkam illiyeṉap poykūṟalum, avaṉ vayiṉ vēṭṭalum - aṅṅaṉam karantu kūṟiya talivaṉi aṭitalit talivi virumputalum, aṉṉa iṭaṅkaḷ alvaḻi ellām - ākiya iṭaṅkaḷ allāta iṭattellām, maṭaṉoṭu niṟṟal kaṭaṉeṉa moḻipa - talivi maṭaṉaḻiyāmal iruttal kaṭami
yeṉak kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
vayiṉ - iṭam. vēṭṭal - virumpal. karattal - oḷittal, maṟittal. eṉavē, taṉvayiṟ karattalum, avaṉ vayiṉ vēṭṭalu mākiya īriṭattum talivi maṭaṉaḻintu kūṟuvaḷeṉpatām.
maṭam - coṉṉati meyyeṉak koṇṭu ati viṭāmal irukkum maṉavaṭakkam. maṭam - maṭaṉ - pōli.
‘kutiri vaḻaṅki varuval’ (kali - 69) eṉat talivaṉ maṟittuk kūṟiyavaḻi, ati meyyeṉak koḷḷutal maṭaṉ; aṅṅaṉam koḷḷātu, ‘aṟintēṉ, parattiyarākiya kutiri eṉpati’ eṉak kūṟutal, maṭaṉaḻital. vaḻaṅkivarutal - ēṟi varutal.
“yāriṉuṅ kātalam eṉṟāṉā ūṭiṉāḷ
  yāriṉum yāriṉum eṉṟu.”	(kuṟaḷ)
talivaṉ,  ‘kātalarkaḷ yāriṉum nam kātalam’ eṉṟati, taṉṉāl (eṉṉāl) kātalikkappaṭṭār yāriṉum uṉṉiṭam kātal mikkuṭiyēṉ eṉak kūṟiṉatākak koṇṭu talivi ūṭik kūṟiyatum maṭaṉaḻintu kūṟiyatēyām.	(55)
12. uṇṭaṟkuriya vallāp poruḷ
101.  uṇṭaṟ kuriya vallāp poruḷi
uṇṭaṉa pōlak kūṟalu marapē.
i - ḷ: uṇṭaṟku uriyavallāta (pacali mutaliyaṉa) poruḷi uṇṭaṉa pōlak kūṟalum muṟiyēyām eṉṟavāṟu.
uyttukkoṇ ṭuṇartalāṉ, uṇṇappaṭutaṟkuriya vallāta (nalaṉ, kuṇam) poruḷip piṟarāl uṇṇappaṭṭatu pōlak kūṟutalumām. marapu - muṟi.
kāṭṭu:
1. “pacaliyāl uṇappaṭṭup paṇṭinīr oḻintakkāl.”	(kali  - 15)
eṉavarum. nīr - taṉmi, aḻaku. taliviyiṉ aḻakip pacali uṇṭatākak kūṟiyatu kāṇka.
2.   “	tōṇala muṇṭu tuṟakkap paṭṭōr
vēṇī ruṇṭa kuṭiyō raṉṉar;
nalkunar purintu nalaṉuṇap paṭṭōr
alkunar pōkiya ūrō raṉṉar;
kūṭiṉar purintu kuṇaṉuṇap paṭṭōr
cūṭuna riṭṭa pūvō raṉṉar.”	(kali - 23)
itaṉuḷ, talivi taṉ kuṇam nalaṉkaḷit talivaṉ uṇṭa tākak kūṟiṉami kāṇka.
       “	pullik kiṭantēṉ puṭipeyarntēṉ avvaḷavil 
aḷḷikkoḷ vaṟṟē pacappu.”	(kuṟaḷ)
pacappu aḷḷik koḷḷumpaṭi niṟintateṉal, ‘kūṟalum’ eṉṉum ummiyāṟ koḷka.	(56)
13. tōḻiyiṉ urimi
102.	tāyatti ṉaṭiyā īyac cellā
viṉivayiṟ ṟaṅkā vīṟṟuk koḷappaṭā
emmeṉa varūuṅ kiḻamit tōṟṟam
alla vāyiṉum pulluva vuḷavē.
i - ḷ: 1. tāyattiṉaṭiyā - peṟṟōruṭiya poruḷkaḷāy makkaḷāl aṭiyamuṭiyātaṉavum, 2. īyac cellā - piṟarkkuk koṭukka avarpāṟ cellātaṉavum, 3. viṉivayiṉ taṅkā - eṭuppup piḷḷikaḷ pōl taṉatākkik koḷḷa muṭiyātaṉavum, 4. vīṟṟuk koḷappaṭā - pakivarāl kavarntu koḷḷap paṭātaṉavum āki, em eṉa varum kiḻamit tōṟṟam - talivi yuṟuppukaḷit tōḻi emmuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟutal, allā vāyiṉum pulluva uḷa - vaḻuvēṉum pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟkup poruntum eṉṟavāṟu.
tāyam -peṟṟōr poruḷ. eṭuppup piḷḷi -  vaḷarppup piḷḷi (cuvīkāram). taṉatākkutal - taṉakkuriya tākkutal (cuvīkarittal). em eṉa varum kiḻami tōṟṟam - tōḻi emmuṭiya eṉṟu collum urimiyiyuṭiya talivi uṟuppukkaḷ. uṟuppup pārkkappaṭutaliṉ, ‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟār.
poruḷ mutaliyaṉa oruvarkkuc conta maṉmiyāl emmuṭiya veṉak kūṟappaṭā. uṟuppukkaḷ tammuṭaṉ kūṭiyaṉa vākiyāl emmeṉak kūṟalāmeṉpatām. oruvar uṟuppukkaḷi maṟṟoruvar evvakiyiṉum aṭiyamuṭiyāmi yaṟika.
kāṭṭu:
      “	oru nāḷeṉ
tōḷnekiḻa puṟṟa tuyarāl tuṇitantōr.”		 (kali - 37)
      “	eṉṟōḷ eḻutiya toyyilum.”	(kali - 18)
eṉat tōḻi, talivi tōḷiṉi eṉ tōḷ eṉṟami kāṇka.
‘uḷa’ eṉṟataṉāl, ciṟupāṉmi talivi tōḻiyuṟuppiṉit taṉṉuṟuppākak kūṟutaluṅ koḷka.
    “	niṉkaṇṇāl kāṇpaṉmaṉ yāṉ.”	(kali - 39)
eṉavarum.	(57)
14. vāḻkkiyuḷ irakkan tōṉṟak kūṟal
103. 	varutta mikuti cuṭṭuṅ kāli
uritteṉa moḻipa vāḻkkiyu ḷirakkam.
i - ḷ: varutta mikuti cuṭṭum kāli - tōḻiyum aṟivarum, parattiyiṟ pirivāl talivaṟkum talivikkum tōṉṟiya mikka varuttattik karutik kūṟumiṭattu , vāḻkkiyuḷ irakkam urittu eṉa moḻipa - avarkaḷ (talivaṉ, talivi) naṭattum ilvāḻkkiyil tamakku irakkan tōṉṟiṟṟākak kūṟalum uritteṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉṟavāṟu.
varutta mikuti - ūṭal. talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirivāl talivi ūṭiṉa viṭattu, ‘nī ivvāṟu ceytal muṟiyaṉṟu’ eṉat tōḻiyum aṟivarum talivaṉik kaṭintu kūṟi nalvaḻip
paṭuttuvar eṉpatām.
vāḻkkiyuḷ irakkamāvatu - nī ivvāṟu ceyyiṉ numatu vāḻkki kēṭuṟumeṉat tamatu varuttan tōṉṟak kūṟutal.
kāṭṭu:
1.   “	nīrnī  ṭāṭiṟ kaṇṇuñ civakkum
 ārntōr vāyiṟ ṟēṉum puḷikkum
 taṇantaṉi yāyi ṉemmuyttuk koṭumō
 antaṇ poyki enti emmūrk
 kaṭumpāmpu vaḻaṅkun teruvil
 naṭuṅkaña revvam kaḷinta vemmē.”	(kuṟun - 354)
itaṉuḷ, ‘illaṟattiṉi nī tuṟantaṉiyāyiṉ emmi emmūrk kaṇṇē viṭuka’ eṉat taṉakku varuttan tōṉṟiṟṟākat tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. ‘emmi’ eṉṟatu, taṉṉiyum taliviyiyum. illaṟattiṉit tuṟattal - taliviyip pirintu parattiyiṭam cellutal.
2.   “	uṭuttum toṭuttum pūṇṭum cerīiyum
taḻiyaṇip  polinta āyamoṭu tuvaṉṟi
viḻavoṭu varuti nīyē, iḵtō
ōrā valcic cīṟil vāḻkkip
perunalak kuṟumakaḷ vanteṉa
iṉiviḻa vākum eṉṉumiv vūrē.”		(kuṟun - 295)
itaṉuḷ, ‘muṉṉar naṭattiya ōrāvalciccīṟil vāḻkki, ivaḷ varap puṟattu viḷiyāṭum viḻavuḷa tāyiṟṟeṉṟu ivvūr kūṟukiṉṟa celvam, ivaḷip pirintāl paḻiya taṉmiyām’ eṉṟu aṟivar iraṅkik kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. ōrāvalci - parukki
yaṟṟa kūḻuṇavu. cīṟil vāḻkki - vaṟumi vāḻvu. iṉiyum nī ivvāṟu naṭantuvariṉ avaḷ tāy vīṭu celvaḷ eṉpatām. (58)
15. uṟaḻuṅ kiḷaviyum aiyak kiḷaviyum
104. 	uyarmoḻik kuriya uṟaḻuṅ kiḷavi
aiyak kiḷavi āṭūuviṟ kurittē.
i - ḷ: uyarmoḻikku uṟaḻum kiḷaviyum uriya - iṉpam uyartaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭattil, oruvar kūṟutaṟku maṟṟoruvar māṟupaṭak kūṟutalum uriya, aiyak kiḷavi āṭūuviṟku urittu - kūṟuvēmō  kūṟēmō eṉṟu aiyamuṟṟuk kūṟutal talivaṟ kurittu eṉṟavāṟu.
uṟaḻtal - māṟupaṭutal. uṟaḻuṅ kiḷavi - talivikkum tōḻikkum uritteṉka.
1. tōḻi uyarmoḻi kūṟiyavaḻit talivi uṟaḻntu kūṟuvaḷ.
2. talivi uyarmoḻi kūṟiyavaḻit tōḻi uṟaḻntu kūṟuvaḷ.
3, 4. talivaṉ uyarmoḻi kūṟiyavaḻit taliviyum tōḻiyum uṟaḻntu kūṟuvar.
kāṭṭu:
1.   “	niṉmukaṅ kāṇu maruntiṉēṉ eṉṉumāl
niṉmukan tāṉpeṟi ṉallatu koṉṉē
maruntu piṟitiyātu millēṉ tiruntiḻāy,
eṉceyvām kolliṉi nām.”			(kali - 60)
eṉat tōḻi kūṟiya vaḻi,
“poṉ ceyvām”
eṉat talivi uṟaḻntu kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
itu, talivaṉatu varuttaṅ kūṟat talivi ati ēṟṟuk koḷḷātu māṟupaṭak kūṟaliṉ (uṟaḻtaliṉ) vaḻuvāy, nāṇa mikutiyāl virivil uṭaṉpaṭāmiyiṉ vaḻuvamiti yāyiṟṟu.
2.   “	ārvuṟṟār neñcam aḻiya viṭuvāṉō
ōrvuṟ ṟorutiṟam olkāta nērkōl
aṟampuri neñcat tavaṉ.”				(kali - 42)
eṉat talivi kūṟutalum,
      “	taṇṇaṟuṅ kōṅka malarnta variyellām
poṉṉaṇi yāṉipōl tōṉṟumē nammaruḷāk
koṉṉāḷaṉ nāṭṭu mali.”			(kali - 42)
eṉat tōḻi uṟaḻntu kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
itu, talivi kūṟṟukku māṟākaliṉ vaḻuvāy, talivaṉ ciṟippuṟamākak kēṭṭu varital payaṉākaliṉ vaḻuvamiti yāyiṟṟu.
3.   “	īta lirantārkkoṉ ṟāṟṟātu vāḻtaliṟ
cātaluṅ kūṭumā maṟṟu;”
eṉat talivaṉ kūṟiya vaḻi,
      “	ivaḷ tanti,
kātaliṉ yārkkuṅ koṭukkum viḻupporuḷ
yātunī vēṇṭiyatu.”				(kali - 61)
eṉat tōḻi uṟaḻntu kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
itu, talivaṉ kūṟṟukku etir kūṟaliṉ vaḻuvāy taliviyiṉ karutti yaṟintu uṭampaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu karututaliṉ vaḻuvamiti yāyiṟṟu.
4.	“colliṉ maṟātīvāḷ maṉṉō ivaḷ.”	(kali - 61)
eṉa, aiyakkiḷavi talivaṟ kuriyavāy vantatu.	(59)


16. tōḻi aṟivuṭiyaḷākak kūṟal
105. 	uṟuka ṇōmpal taṉṉiyal pākaliṉ
uriya tākun talivaṟ kuriyavāy uraṉē.
i - ḷ: uṟukaṇ ōmpal taṉ iyalpu ākaliṉ - talivikku vanta varuttattip pōkkutal tōḻikkuk kaṭaṉātaliṉ, tōḻikaṇ uraṉ uriyatākum - tōḻi aṟivuṭiyaḷākak kūṟutalum urittākum eṉṟavāṟu.
uṟukaṇ - tuṉpam. uraṉ- aṟivuṭimi. atāvatu, talivaṉ piriyak karutiṉ appiriviṉāl payaṉilliyeṉak kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭi vilakkutal.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ’ talivi uraṉuṭiyaḷākak kūṟaluṅ koḷka.
kāṭṭu:
       “	piṇṭa nelliṉ aḷḷū raṉṉaveṉ
oṇṭoṭi nekiḻiṉum nekiḻka
ceṉṟī perumaniṟ ṟakikkunar yārē.”	              (akam - 46)
itaṉuḷ, talivaṉ cellāmi yaṟitaliṉ, ‘talivi varuntiṉum nī cel’ eṉa, tōḻi uraṉuṭiyaḷākak kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. talivi kūṟṟuk kilakkiyam vantuḻik kāṇka.	(60)
17. tōḻi uyarmoḻik kiḷavi kūṟal
106.	uyarmoḻik kiḷaviyu muriyavā lavaṭkē.
i - ḷ: avaṭku - tōḻikku, uyarmoḻik kiḷaviyum uriya - talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum uyarttuk kūṟutalum uriyavām eṉṟavāṟu. uyarttal - mikac ciṟappittal. orōvōriṭattu uriyaveṉka.
kāṭṭu:
       “	makiḻmikac ciṟappa mayaṅkiṉaḷ kollō
yāṇa rūraniṉ māṇiḻi yarivi
kāviri malirniṟi yaṉṉaniṉ
mārpunaṉi vilakkal toṭaṅki yōḷē.”		 (aiṅ - 42)
itaṉuḷ, kāvirip perukkup pōlat taliviyi nōkki varukiṉṟa talivaṉ mārpiṉi avaḷ vilakkumāṟu eṉṉeṉat taliviyi uyarttuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. mārpiṉi vilakkutal - pirintu āṟṟiyiruttal. āṟṟiyiruttalil vallaḷ eṉat taliviyic ciṟappittavāṟu.
      “	ulakam
purappāṉ pōlvatōr matukiyu muṭiyāṉ.”	(kali - 47)
itu, talivaṉi uyarttuk kūṟiyatu.	(61)


18. maṅkala mutaliya moḻikaḷ
107. 	maṅkala moḻiyum viya moḻiyum
māṟil āṇmiyiṟ colliya moḻiyum
kūṟiyal maruṅkiṟ koḷḷu meṉpa.	
i-ḷ: maṅkala moḻiyum - talivaṟkut tīṅku varumeṉ ṟañcit taliviyum tōḻiyum avaṉi vāḻttutalum, 2. viya moḻiyum - talivaṉ tammi vañcittāṉākat taliviyum tōḻiyuṅ kūṟutalum; viya moḻi - tīṅki uḷvitta moḻi, vañcaṉi. 3. māṟil āṇmiyil colliya moḻiyum - māṟu
pāṭillāta taṉatu āṇmiyil tavaṟiṉāṉ  eṉak kūṟutalum, kūṟu iyal maruṅkil koḷḷum eṉpa - vaḻuvamitiyākak kūṟiya ilakkaṇattiṭattē koḷḷum moḻi eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
  āṇmi - kuṟṟaṅ kaḷintu kuṇaṅ kikkoṇṭu naṭattal. atāvatu, nalloḻukkatti yāḷutal. āṇmi
yiṉmi - nalloḻukkattiṟ ṟavaṟi naṭattal.
1. maṅkala moḻi - piriviṭattum, 2. viya moḻi - pirivu nīṭṭippiṉum, 3. āṇmiyil col - talivaṉ valitiṟ pukutal mutaliya tavaṟu ceyta viṭattum kūṟuvaṉavām. 
kāṭṭu:
1. “nōyila rākanaṅ kātalar”			           (akam - 115)
itu, nammi aṟaṉaṉṟit tuṟattaliṉ tīṅku varumeṉṟañci vāḻttiyatu. namporuṭṭu avarkkut tīṅku varumeṉṟu niṉittaliṉ vaḻuvamintatu.
2. “viyiṉar nalaṉuṇṭār vārāmi niṉittaliṉ.”       (kali - 184)
itu, vañcittami kūṟiṟṟu. viyiṉar - vañcakar. varuvatākak kūṟi nalaṉi yuṇṭu vārāmiyiṉ vañcakar eṉṟār.
3.   “	yārivaṉ eṅkūntal koḷvāṉ ituvumōr
ūrāṇmik kotta paṭiṟuṭit temmaṉi
vāralnī vantāṅkē māṟu.”			(kali - 89)
itu, āṇmiyil paḻiyuṇṭu eṉṟatu. valiya eṉatu kūntalip piṭittal uṉatu oḻukkattiṟku ēṟṟatalla eṉak kūṟutal kāṇka. paṭiṟu - koṭumi.	(62)
19. meyppāṭṭamiti
108. 	ciṉaṉē pētimi nimpiri nalkura
vaṉināl vakiyum ciṟappoṭu varumē.	
itu, meyppāṭṭiyalil (cū - 26) ākāveṉpavaṟṟuḷ cila ivviṭattu amiyumeṉa vaḻuvamikkiṉṟatu. potu
vākak kūṟaliṉ talivikkum tōḻikkuṅ koḷka.
i - ḷ: ciṉam, pētimi, nimpiri, nalkuravu eṉṉum ivi nāṉkum kātalic ciṟappittuk kūṟumiṭattu varum eṉṟavāṟu.
ciṉam - ciṉam, nīṭṭittal. pētimi - aṟiviṉmi. nimpiri- poṟāmi. nalkuravu - celvamiṉmi.
kāṭṭu:
1.   “	toṭiya vemakkunī yāri? periyārk
kaṭiyarō āṟṟā tavar.”				(kali - 88)
itaṉuḷ, ‘eṉṉit toṭātē; emakku nī yār? piriyavalla periyārkkup pirivāṟṟātavar (taliviyar) aṭiyarō?’ eṉac ciṉantu kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. itu ciṉam paṟṟi variṉum, taṉ kātalic ciṟappittaliṉ amintatu.
2.   “	cevviya tīviya colli, yavaṟṟoṭu
piya muyaṅkiya aññāṉ ṟaviyellām
poyyātal yāṉyāṅ kaṟikōmaṟ ṟiya.”		(kali - 19)
itaṉuḷ, yāṉ kaḻiperuṅkātalāl niṉṉi uḷḷavāṟaṟin tilēṉ eṉat talivi taṉ pētimiyik kātalāl ciṟappittami kāṇka. ‘yāṉ yāṅku aṟikō’ eṉṟatu, yāṉ mikka kātalāl pētimiyuṟṟu aṉṟu nī ceytaviyellām poyyeṉ ṟaṟiyāmaṟ pōṉē ṉeṉpatām.
3.   “ 	uṟaliyām oḷivāṭa uyarntavaṉ viḻaviṉuḷ
viṟaliḻi yavaroṭu viḷiyāṭu vāṉmaṉṉō
peṟalarum poḻutoṭu piṟaṅkiṇart turutticūḻn
taṟalvārum viyiyeṉ ṟaṟiyuna ruḷarāyiṉ.”	(kali - 30)
itaṉuḷ, avarōṭum viḷiyāṭuvāṉeṉap poṟāmi tōṉṟak kūṟiyum, avaṉiṅku varavēṇṭumeṉak kātalic ciṟappittavāṟu kāṇka. uṟal + yām - uṟaliyām - taṉṉi uṟutaṟkuriya yām.
4.   “	aṟivum oḻukkamum yāṇṭuṇarn taṉaḷkol
koṇṭa koḻunaṉ kuṭivaṟa ṉuṟṟeṉak
koṭutta tāti koḻuñcō ṟuḷḷāḷ
oḻukunīr nuṇaṅkaṟal pōlap
poḻutumaṟut tuṇṇuñ ciṟumatu kiyaḷē.”	(naṟ - 110)
itaṉuḷ, ‘kuṭivaṟaṉuṟṟu’ eṉa nalkuravu kūṟiyum, ‘koḻuñ cōṟu uḷḷāḷ, poḻutumaṟuttu uṇṇum, aṟivum oḻukkamum yāṇṭuṇarntaṉaḷ’ eṉak kātalic ciṟappittaliṉ amintatu.	(63)
20. kāṭṭalākāp poruḷ
109. oppum uruvum veṟuppu meṉṟā
 kaṟpum ērum eḻilu meṉṟā
 cāyalum nāṇum maṭaṉu meṉṟā
 nōyum vēṭkiyum nukarvu meṉṟāṅ
 kāvayiṉ varūuṅ kiḷavi yellām 
 nāṭṭiya marapiṉ neñcukoḷi ṉallatu
 kāṭṭa lākāp poruḷa veṉpa.
itu, kāṭcip poru ḷallāta karuttup poruḷiyum kāṭcip poruḷ pōlap poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum eṉkiṉṟatu.
i - ḷ: oppu mutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭum, avi pōṉṟa piṟavum - nāṭṭiya marapiṉ neñcu koḷiṉ allatu -  pulaṉeṟi vaḻakka muṟiyāṉē uṇarvatallātu, kāṭṭal ākāp poruḷa eṉpa - ulaka vaḻakkāṉ oruvarkkoruvar kaṭpulaṉākak kāṭṭap paṭāta poruḷa eṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
maṉavuṇarvā ṉaṉṟip poṟiyāṉ uṇarappaṭā veṉpatām. ‘eṉṟā’ eṉpaṉa eṇṇiṭic coṟkaḷ.
1. 	oppu - otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum (kaḷa -3) eṉṟavaḻi, avaroppumiyi maṉavuṇarvāṉaṉṟi meyvēṟu pāṭu paṟṟip poṟiyāṉ uṇaralākā teṉpatu. atāvatu, kaṇṇāl pārttu ivaṉum ivaḷum oppar eṉak kūṟamuṭiyā teṉpatām. ‘tantiyar oppar makkaḷ’ eṉpatu matu.
2. 	uru - accam. atuvum poṟinutal viyarttal pōlvaṉa vaṟṟālaṉṟi, vaṭivu paṟṟi uṇaralākātu.
3. 	veṟuppu - ceṟivu, maṉaviṟukkam. atuvum poṟiyālu ṇaralākātu.
4. 	kaṟpu - maṉivikkuk kaṇavaṉiṭattu ēṟpaṭum aṉpuṇarcci. itu kaṇavaṟkum okkum.
5. 	ēr - eḻucci; maṉaveḻucciyālēṟpaṭum uṭaleḻucci. itu, taṉṉiyaṟiyāmal nikaḻvatām. itu, eḻukiṉṟa nilimi eṉa nikaḻkālamē kuṟittu niṟkum.
6. 	eḻil - aṅṅaṉam vaḷarntaminta paruvattum, itu vaḷarntu māṟiya taṉṟi iṉṉum vaḷaru meṉpatu pōṉṟu kāṭṭutal. atāvatu, eḻucciyā lēṟpaṭum aḻakuṇarcci yeṉka.
7. 	cāyal - aimpoṟiyāl nukarum meṉmi; meṉmit taṉmiyākum.
8. 	nāṇ - ceyyat takātaṉavaṟṟic ceyya nāṇutal.
9. 	maṭaṉ -koḷuttak koṇṭu koṇṭatu viṭāmi. atāvatu, aṭakkam, koḷutta - colla.
10. 	nōy - nōtal.
11. vēṭki- poruḷkaḷmēl tōṉṟum paṟṟuḷḷam; kāṇum poruḷ kaḷiyellām taṉakkuriyaṉavāka eṇṇutal.
12. 	nukarvu - iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷi nukarum nukarcci.
piṟavāvaṉa: oḷiyum aḷiyum kāytalum aṉpum aḻukkāṟum poṟiyum niṟiyum aṟivum mutaliyaṉa.
1. 	oḷi - aṟivu nuṇukkam; veḷḷaṟiviṉmi eṉka.
2. 	aḷi - aṉpu kāraṇattāl tōṉṟum aruḷ.
3. 	kāytal - vekuḷi.
4. 	aṉpu - maṉimakkaḷ mutaliya cuṟṟattāriṭam maṉamakiḻcci yuṇṭākkip piṇittu niṟkum uṇarcci.
5. 	aḻukkāṟu - piṟar poruḷ mutaliya kaṇṭu poṟāmi. aḻukkāṟu - poṟāmi.
6. 	poṟi - piṟar ceyta tīṅkip poṟuttal.
7. 	niṟi - maṟi piṟar aṟiyāmal oḻukutal. maṟi - kaḷavoḻukkam.
8. 	aṟivu - nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ tīmiyum uḷḷa vāṟaṟital. ivi eṭṭum kāṇappaṭāp poruḷēṉum poruḷākak koḷka eṉpatām. ivi akattum puṟattum varum. puṟattil varumāṟu:
kāṭṭu:
      “	varipuri maḻakaḷiṟṟiṉ mici.”		     (puṟam - 53)  oppu
      “	veṟutta kēḷvi viḷaṅkupukaḻk kapilaṉ.”  (puṟam - 53) veṟuppu
      “	urukeḻu muracam.”				(puṟam - 50)
uru eṉa varum.	(64)
21. aṟamporuḷiṉpam
110. 	imiyōr tēettum eṟikaṭal varippiṉum
aviyal kālam iṉmi yāṉa.
i-ḷ: tēvarulakattum maṇṇulakattum aṟam poruḷiṉ paṅkaḷiṉ nukarvillāta kālam illi eṉṟavāṟu. eṅkum eṉṟum uṇṭeṉpatu.
eṉavē, aṟamporuḷiṉpaṅkaḷum kāṭṭalākāp poruḷkaḷēyā meṉpatām.
tēm - iṭam. eṟikaṭal varippu - alikaṭal cūḻnta ulakam. tēvarulakam - ōriṭam. (tolkāppiyar kālat tamiḻar - ‘teyvam’ eṉpatu pārkka.)	(65)
7. vaḻakku
1. vaḻakkilakkaṇam
111. 	vaḻakkeṉap paṭuva tuyarntōr mēṟṟē
nikaḻcci yavarkaṭ ṭāka lāṉa.
i - ḷ: nikaḻcci avarkaṭku ākalāṉa - uyarntōr āṇip paṭi ulakam nikaḻvatāl, vaḻakku eṉappaṭuvatu uyarntōr mēṟṟē- vaḻakkeṉṟu collappaṭuvatu uyarntōr vaḻaṅkiya vaḻakkē yākum eṉṟavāṟu.
kaṭṭu - kaṇ, iṭam. uyarntōr - mēṉmakkaḷ. uyarntōr vaḻakkiṉpaṭi  naṭappamē makkaḷ kaṭami eṉpatām. naṭakkiyē yaṉṟi moḻivaḻakkum vaḻakkeṉavē paṭum.	(66)
2. ceyyuḷ vaḻakku
112. uyarntōr kiḷaviyum vaḻakkoṭu puṇartaliṉ
 vaḻakkuvaḻip paṭuttal ceyyuṭkuk kaṭaṉē.
i - ḷ: uyarntōr kiḷaviyum vaḻakkoṭu puṇartaliṉ - uyar makkaḷ  kūṟum ulaka vaḻakkac coṟkaḷum ceyyuṭkup porunti varutaliṉ, vaḻakku vaḻippaṭuttal ceyyuṭkuk kaṭaṉē - avvulaka vaḻakkiṉ vaḻiyilē naṭattal ceyyuṭku muṟimiyākum  eṉṟavāṟu.
uyarntōr vaḻaṅkum ulaka vaḻakkappaṭiyē ceyyuḷ ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatām. ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṟ kuriya kaṟpaṉi nīṅka niṟpavi ulaka vaḻakkāy irukkac ceytalē ceyyuḷākum eṉpatu.
akattiṇiyiyalil, ‘nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum’ (akat - 42) iyaṉṟatu ceyyuḷ vaḻakkeṉṟār. īṇṭu, avvulaka vaḻakkam uyarntōr vaḻakkeṉa ataṉ ciṟappuk kūṟiṉār.
“nāṟcol liyalāṉ yāppuvaḻip paṭṭaṉa.”	(cey - 80)
“ceyyuḷ moḻiyāṉ cīrpuṉin tiyāppiṉ.”	(cey - 236)
eṉpavaṟṟāl, ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷē ceyyuṭ collāvatāl, uyarntōr vaḻaṅkiya vaḻakkēyaṉṟic coṟkaḷum ceyyuṭkātal peṟappaṭum.	(67)
3. aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉa
113. 	aṟakkaḻi vuṭiyaṉa poruṭpayam paṭavariṉ
  vaḻakkeṉa vaḻaṅkalum paḻittaṉa ṟeṉpa.
i-ḷ: aṟakkaḻivu uṭiyaṉa - ulaka vaḻakkiṟkup poruttamillāta karuttukkaḷ, poruḷ payam paṭavariṉ - akapporuṭkup payaṉuṭiyavāka varumāyiṉ, vaḻakku eṉa vaḻaṅkalum paḻittaṉṟu eṉpa - avaṟṟi vaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkutalum kuṟṟamuṭittaṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉṟavāṟu.
aṟakkaḻivu - oḻukkattavaṟu. atāvatu, vaḻakki mīṟi varuvatu. poruṭpayampaṭa varutalāvatu - talivaṉic celavaḻuṅku
vittal, taliviyi āṟṟuvittal mutaliya kāriya muṭikkap payaṉpaṭutal.
aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉa vāvaṉa: tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattil talivaṉ kuṟiyuṟṟu niṉṟatit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟumpōtu, talivaṉ taṉṉi virumpiṉāṉ eṉṟu kūṟutalum, talivi talivaṉuḷḷa iṭattic ceṉṟaṭitalum, niṉ ūrkkuc celvāmeṉat talivi kūṟutalum (kaḷa - 45) pōlvaṉa.
kāṭṭu:
1.   “	ciṟupuṟaṅ kaviyiṉa ṉāka ataṟkoṇ
ṭikupeyal maṇṇiṉ ñekiḻpaña ruṟṟaveṉ
uḷḷava ṉaṟita lañci yuḷḷil
kaṭiya kūṟik kipiṇi viṭā.”		              (akam - 32)
eṉat talivaṉ taṉṉi virumpiṉāṉ eṉat tōḻi koṇṭāḷ pōlum eṉat talivi karutumāṟṟāl tōḻi kūṟavē, talivi maṟi pulappaṭuttuvaḷeṉpatu payaṉāyiṟṟu.
2.   “	maḷḷar kuḻīiya viḻavi ṉāṉum
makaḷir kuḻīiya tuṇaṅki yāṉum
yāṇṭuṅ kāṇēṉ māṇṭak kōṉi.”	              (kuṟun - 31)
itu, talivi tēṭic ceṉṟatu.
3.  “ aruṅkaṭi yaṉṉi kāval nīvip
peruṅkaṭi yiṟantu maṉṟam pōkip
pakalē palaruṅ kāṇa nāṇviṭ
ṭakalvayaṟ paṭappi yavaṉūr viṉavic
ceṉmō vāḻi tōḻi.”				(naṟ - 365)
itu, talivi celvām eṉṟatu.
tēṭic ceṉṟatum, celvām eṉṟatum aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉa vēṉum, ciṟippuṟamāka varivu kaṭātalākiya poruṭpayaṉ tarutaliṉ amintatu. tōḻi talivi iruvarkkum koḷka.(68)
4. mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi
114. 	mikka poruḷiṉuṭ poruḷvaki puṇarkka
nāṇuttalip piriyā nalvaḻip paṭuttē.
i - ḷ: mikka poruḷiṉuḷ - avvāṟu aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉa kūṟumpōtu, nāṇuttalip piriyā nalvaḻip poruḷvaki paṭuttup puṇarkka - taliviyatu nāṇ nīṅkāmik kētuvākiya nalla poruṭ kūṟupāṭukaḷi uḷḷaṭakkik kūṟuka eṉṟavāṟu.
taliviyatu nāṇ nīṅkāmal aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉa kūṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatām. mikka poruḷ - vaḻakkik kaṭanta poruḷ, aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉa.
atāvatu, tōḻi, ‘nēṟṟu nāṉ kākkiṉṟa puṉattu oruvaṉ vantu puṉcoṟkūṟi eṉṉit taḻuviṉāṉ; nāṉ avaṉi vaṉcoṟkūṟi nīkkiṉēṉ; iṉṟum varuvāṉ. nām avaṉik kaṇṭu cirippām. nī avaṉ varumiṭattē celka’ eṉavē, talivaṉi ivaḷ vēṟākak koṇṭāḷ eṉat talivi karutumāṟṟāl kūṟiṉā ḷēṉum, ‘ivaḷ eṉakkuc ciṟantā
ḷeṉpati aṟintē taḻuviṉāṉ; ivaḷ avaṉuṭaṉ eṉṉik kūṭṭavē eṉṉi vēṟu niṟutti āyattōṭu pirintaṉaḷ’ eṉat talivi piritaliṉ nāṇ piriyāmi aṟika. ‘neruna lelli’ (akam - 32) pārkka.	(69)
8. muṟippeyar
1. ellā
115. 	muṟippeyar maruṅkiṟ keḻutakip potuccol
nilikkuri marapiṉ iruvīṟṟu murittē.
i - ḷ: muṟippeyar maruṅkil keḻutakip potuccol - muṟippeyariṭattu irupāṟkum poruntiṉa takutiyiyuṭiya ‘ellā’ eṉṉum col, nilikkuri marapiṉ iruvīṟṟum urittē - pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟkuriya muṟimiyiṉālē āṇpāṟkum peṇpāṟkum oppa uriyatāy vaḻaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.
āṇpālukkum peṇpālukkum potuvāy vaḻaṅkum ‘ellā’ eṉṉum col, āṇpālip peṇpālum, peṇ pāli āṇpālum kūṟaṟkuriyatākum eṉpatām. keḻu - poruntiya. taki - takuti. nili - pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku.
tōḻiyum taliviyum talivaṉi ‘ellā’ eṉavum, talivaṉ taliviyiyum tōḻiyiyum ciṟupāṉmi pāṅkaṉiyum ‘ellā’ eṉavum kūṟuvar. 
ituvē iṉṟu cilar maṉiviyi, ‘ēllā’ eṉṟu aḻippatākum. itu, ‘elā, ella, eluva’ eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
kāṭṭu:
1.   “	iṉi ellā! yām
tītilēm eṉṟu teḷippavum.”	(kali - 81)
eṉat talivaṉ taliviyi ‘ellā’ eṉṟāṉ.
2.   “	ellā nī
muṉṉarttā ṉoṉṟu kuṟittāṟpōṟ kāṭṭiṉi.”	(kali - 61)
eṉat tōḻi talivaṉi ‘ellā’ eṉṟāḷ.	(70)
2. aṉṉi, eṉṉi
116.	 aṉṉi eṉṉi yeṉṟalu muḷavē
toṉṉeṟu muṟimi colliṉum eḻuttiṉum
tōṉṟā marapiṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar.
i - ḷ: colliṉum eḻuttiṉum tōṉṟā marapiṉa - colla tikārattum eḻuttatikārattum collappaṭāta ilakkaṇat taṉavāy, tolneṟi muṟimi - pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟkup poruntiya paḻiya neṟimuṟiyāṉē, aṉṉi eṉṉi eṉṟalum uḷa eṉmaṉār pulavar - tōḻi taliviyi aṉṉi eṉṟalum, talivi tōḻiyi aṉṉi eṉṟalum, iruvarum talivaṉi eṉṉi eṉṟalum uḷaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
kāṭṭu:
1.   “	aṉṉāy ivaṉōr iḷamā ṇākkaṉ.”	             (kuṟun - 33)
itu, tōḻiyit talivi aṉṉi eṉṟatu. aṉṉi - aṉṉāy  - viḷi.
2.   “	aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi nammūrp
pārppaṉak kuṟumakap pōlat tāmum
kuṭumit taliya maṉṟa
neṭumali nāṭa ṉūrnta māvē.”		              (aiṅ - 202)
itu, taliviyit tōḻi aṉṉi eṉṟatu.
3.   “	eṉakku mākā teṉṉikku mutavātu.”	             (kuṟun - 27)
itu, talivaṉit talivi eṉṉi eṉṟatu.
      “	eṉṉik kūriḵ taṉmi yāṉum
eṉṉikku nāṭiḵ taṉmi yāṉum.”		(puṟam - 85)
eṉa, eṉṉi eṉpatu puṟattiṟkum varum.	(71)
3. kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti
117.  orupāṟ kiḷavi eṉippāṟ kaṇṇum
varuvaki tāṉē vaḻakkeṉa moḻipa.
itu, ‘otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum’ (kaḷa- 3) eṉpataṟkup puṟaṉaṭi.
i - ḷ: orupāṟ kiḷavi - ‘otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum’ eṉṟa āṇorumiyum peṇṇorumiyum kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ, eṉippāṟkaṇṇum varuvaki tāṉē - paṉmip poruḷu ṇarttutal, vaḻakku eṉa moḻipa - ulaka vaḻakkeṉṟu collupa āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉippāl - āṇum peṇṇum ākiya uyartiṇip palarpāl.
atavātu, ovvoru nilattum pala talivarum pala taliviyarum uṇmiyāl, avarkaḷi yellām kiḻavarkaḷ, kiḻattikaḷ eṉap paṉmiyāl kūṟāmal, kiḻavaṉ, kiḻatti eṉak kūṟum orumic collē appaṉmiyik kuṟikkum eṉpatām.
oruvaṉukkum oruttikkum kūṟum ilakkaṇamē ellāt talivarkkum taliviyarkkum okkum eṉka. kiḻavaṉ, kiḻatti eṉpataṉṟi, kiḻavar, kiḻattiyar eṉṉum vaḻakkiṉmi aṟika.	(72)
4. mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi
118.  ellā vuyirkkum iṉpa meṉpatu
  tāṉamarntu varūu mēvaṟ ṟākum.
i - ḷ: iṉpam eṉpatutāṉ - aṟamum poruḷum oḻiya iṉpam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu vatāṉatu, ellā uyirkkum amarntu varum mēvaṟṟākum - makkaḷum māvum puḷḷum mutalākavuḷḷa ellā uyirkaḷiṭattum āṇ, peṇ eṉṉum iru kūṟṟiṉkaṭ porunti nikaḻvatākum eṉṟavāṟu. amartal - poruntutal.
eṉṟatu, āṇum peṇṇumāy iṉpa nukarntu varutaliṉ, oruvaṉum oruttiyumē iṉpa nukarntār eṉappaṭātu. avviṉpam ellāvuyirkkum potu eṉpatūum, uyir
kaḷellām āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālāyp puṇarcci nikaḻttum eṉpatūum kūṟiyavāṟām. iṉpam ellā uyirkkum potuvātal pōla, aṟamum poruḷum makkaṭkaṉṟi maṟṟiyuyirkaṭ killi.
ellā vuyirum āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālākavē iṉpa nukartaliṉ, kiḻavaṉ, kiḻatti eṉṉum orumi, ellāt talivar taliviyariyuṅ kuṟittup paṉmiyāyiyalum eṉa vaḻuvamittavāṟu.	(73)

iraṇṭāvatu potuviyal
kuḻantiyuri
muṟṟiṟṟu.
* * *


mūṉṟāvatu
kaḷaviyal
1. kaḷaviṉ vaki
119. 	kāmap puṇarcciyum iṭantalip pāṭum
pāṅkoṭu taḻālun tōḻiyiṟ puṇarvumeṉ
ṟāṅkanāl vakiyiṉum aṭinta cārpoṭu
maṟiyeṉa moḻital maṟiyō rāṟē.
ivviyal, kaḷavu kaṟpeṉṉum akavoḻukkam iraṇṭaṉuḷ, kaḷaviṉatu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟamiyāṉ kaḷaviyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. kaḷavu - kaḷavoḻukkam. iyal - ilakkaṇam.
muṉṉiraṇṭu iyalkaḷil kaḷavu kaṟpu eṉṉum iruvakik kikōḷkaḷiṉ potuvilakkaṇaṅ kūṟi, avviru vakiyuḷ muṉṉiṉṟa kaḷavilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvatāl, itu muṉṉiyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu. potu vilakkaṇaṅkūṟic ciṟappilakkaṇaṅ kūṟutal muṟiyātalaṟika.
immutal nūṟpā, kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ vaki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
i-ḷ: iyaṟkip puṇarcciyum iṭantalippāṭum pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamum tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭamum eṉa annāṉku vakiyiṉ kaṇṇum naṭappatu kaḷavoḻukkam eṉṟu kūṟutal kāma nūlār muṟiyākum eṉṟavāṟu.
kāmappuṇarcci - iyaṟkip puṇarcci. taḻāl - taḻuvutal. puṇarvu - kūṭṭam. maṟi - kaḷavoḻukkam. maṟiyōr - kaḷavu nūl vallār. maṟintoḻukaliṉ kaḷavu - maṟi eṉappaṭṭatu. aṭinta cārpoṭu - poruntiya cārpiyuṭiyatu maṟi eṉṟapaṭi. annāṉku kūṟṟiṉum porunti naṭappateṉpatām. āṟu - muṟi.
1. iyaṟkip puṇarcci	3. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam
2. iṭantalippāṭu	4. tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam
eṉum innāṉkum kaḷaviṉ vakiyākum. 	(1)
2. iyaṟkip puṇarcci
120.	iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉu meṉṟāṅ 
kaṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṉ
kāmak kūṭṭaṅ kāṇuṅ kāli
maṟiyōr tēettu maṉṟa leṭṭaṉuḷ
tuṟiyami nalliyāḻt tuṇimiyō riyalpē.
niṟutta muṟiyāṉē iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu innūṟpā.
i - ḷ: iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟāṅku - iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟa im mūvakipporuḷ
kaḷuḷ, aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṉ - oruvaṉ orutti yiṭattut tōṉṟiya iṉpattiṉ pakutiyākiya puṇartal mutaliya aivaki oḻukkattiṉuḷ, kāmak kūṭṭam kāṇum kāli - iyaṟkip puṇarcciyi ārāyuṅ kālattu, maṟiyōr tēettu maṉṟal eṭṭaṉuḷ - āriyariṭattu nikaḻum eṇvaki maṇaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya, kantaruvar oḻukkattip pōṉṟatē.
kāmak kūṭṭam - iyaṟkip puṇarcci. maṟiyōr - āriyar. ‘vētiyar’ eṉpataṉ tamiḻ moḻi peyarppu. tēettu - iṭattu. maṉṟal - maṇam. tuṟi ami nal yāḻ  paṇṇaminta nallayāḻ. yāḻōr - tamiḻici valla oru kuṟiñci vakuppār. yāḻ - ici. puṇartal eṉṉum oḻukkam kuṟiñcikkaṇ nikaḻvatālum, ici kātaliṉpatti vaḷarkkumoṉ ṟākiyālum, kuṟiñci vāṇarākiya avviciyāḷarik kaḷavukkuriyarākak kūṟutal marapu. kantaru varum icivallā reṉpatāl, avvoppumi paṟṟi, avarit tamiḻp peyarāl ‘yāḻōr’ eṉṟār. tuṇimiyōr - oruvaṉum oruttiyum.
āciriyar tolkāppiyar nūl ceyta kālattē tamiḻ makkaḷ āriyar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟit terintiruntaṉar. eṉavē, iyaṟkip puṇarcciyō ṭorupuṭi oppumiyuṭiya kantaruva maṇatti, iyaṟkip puṇarccikku uvamiyāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar.
āriya maṇam eṭṭaṉuḷ kantaruva maṇam eṉpatu, iyaṟkip puṇarcci pōlavē, oruvaṉum oruttiyum tāmē etirppaṭṭuk kūṭip pirivatu; piṉṉar avviruvarum oṉṟu kūṭuvatilli; atāvatu, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟil etirppaṭṭuk kātal mutirntu maṇañ ceytu koḷvatilli; kaṇṭavar kaṇṭaviṭattuk kūṭip pirintu celvar. āṉāl, tamiḻ makkaḷ iyaṟkip puṇarccikkup piṉ kaḷavoḻukkam oḻuki maṇañceytu koṇṭu vāḻkki naṭattuvar. iyaṟkip puṇarcciyil perumpālum uḷḷap puṇarcciyē nikaḻum. uḷḷap puṇarcci - maṉattāl oruvari yoruvar kātalittu aṉpu koḷḷutal, maṉa vorumi yaṭital. kantaruvamō meyyuṟṟup puṇarvatākum. iyaṟkip puṇarcci kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ mutaṟpaṭi. kantaruvam mutalum muṭivum atuvē. iyaṟkip puṇarcci oḻukkam. kantaruvam maṇam. eṉiṉum, iyaṟkip puṇarcciyum kantaruvamum oruvaṉum oruttiyum tāmē etirppaṭṭup pirital eṉṉum orukū ṟottalāṉ, avviraṇṭum orupuṭi oppuṭiyateṉpatu kuṟittu, ‘yāḻt tuṇimiyō riyalpē’ eṉṟār. ‘kantaruvam pōṉṟatu iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉa muḻutuñ cēṟalāka oppiṭṭuk kūṟāmiyum aṟika.
paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṭip paṇṭu nikaḻntu vanta ivvetirp pāṭāṉatu, pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkil - kāmak kūṭṭam, kāmappuṇarcci, iyaṟkip puṇarcci, muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci, teyvap puṇarcci eṉap pala peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkum. kāmak kūṭṭam - aṉpāl kūṭuṅkūṭṭam. kāmap puṇarcci - kāmak kūṭṭam. puṇarcci - kūṭṭam. iyaṟkip puṇarcci - kūṭṭuvāriṉṟi oruvaṉum oruttiyum tāmē kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam. muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci - kātaṟ perukkāl kūṭutal. muṉṉuṟutal - perukutal. teyvap puṇarcci - ciṟanta, mēṉmiyāṉa kūṭṭam.
aintiṇi - iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum ākiyavā ṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, 1. puṇartal mutalākiya aintum iṉpantarutaliṉ iṉpamāyiṉa. 2. mulli mutaliya nāṉku tiṇikaḷukkum nilamum kālamum karupporuḷum, kuṟiñci mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ puṟattavākiya veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi eṉpaṉavum, vākiyuḷ mutaṟ pakutiyum poruḷākalāṉum, puṇartal mutaliya uripporuḷkaḷ kēṭiṉmiyāṉum ivi poruḷāyiṉa. 3. ivvoḻukkam aintum aṟattiṉ vaḻi nikaḻtalāṉum, pālip puṟattatāṉa vakiyuḷ aṟaṉili kūṟutalāṉum ivi aṟamāyiṉa. (puṟat tiṇiyiyal 2ām cūttira uri pārkka.)
eṉavē, kaḷavoḻukka moḻukik kātal mutirntu maṇañ ceytu koṇṭu poruḷīṭṭi aṟantalip piriyātu iṉpa nukartalē vāḻkkip payaṉ eṉpatu.
iṉpaṅ karutiya kaḷavoḻukkan toṭaṅkip poruḷāl avvoḻukkam valiyuṟṟup piṉṉar illaṟa vāḻkkiyāka naṭipeṟutaṟkuriyatu eṉa, iṉpavoḻukkattiṉ ciṟappum, iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ ilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟiṟṟu iccūttiram.
āriya maṇam eṭṭāvaṉa
1. piramam	5. kantaruvam
2. piracāpattiyam 	6. acuram
3. āriṭam	7. irākkatam
4. teyvam	8. picācam                eṉpaṉa.
1. piramam: otta kōttirattāṉāy, 48 yāṇṭu piramacariyaṅ kāttāṉukku, 12 yāṇṭup paruvamuḷḷa pūppeyti yāḷi, iraṇṭāmpūp peytu muṉṉar aṇikalaṉaṇintu koṭiyākak koṭuppatu. piramacariyam - maṇamiṉṟik kaṟkum nili.
2. piracāpattiyam: maṇamakaṉ peṟṟōr koṭutta paricattip pōl irumaṭaṅku koṭuttup peṇṇik koṭuppatu. iṉṟu tamiḻ vakuppiṉariṭi naṭipeṟum tirumaṇaṅkaḷ ippiracāpattiyap pōliyē yākum. paricam - pariyam. māppiḷḷi vīṭṭāriṭam pariyam vāṅkutalum, peṇṇukkuc cīrkoṭuttalum iṉṟu ellāt tamiḻ vakuppāriṭiyum naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
3. āriṭam: āṇ peṇ māṭukaḷiraṇṭiṉ kompukaḷukkum kuḷampukaḷukkum poṟpūṇaṇintu, avaṟṟiṉ naṭuvil maṇa makkaḷi niṟuttip poṉpūṭṭi, ‘nīvirum ivipōṟ polintu vāḻvīrāka’ eṉa nīrvārttup peṇṇik koṭuppatu.
4. teyvam: vēḷvi vēṭpikkum āciriyaṉukku, vēḷvit tī muṉṉarp peṇṇik kāṇikkiyākak koṭuppatu.
ivi nāṉkum āriyap pirāmaṇarukkē uriyavām. acuramum irākkatamum perumpālum aracarkkē uriyaṉa. picācam cūttirark kuriyatām. kantaruvam ellārkkum potu.
5. kantaruvam: oruvaṉum oruttiyum etirppaṭṭa iṭattil kūṭippirivatu. parācarar maccakantiyik kūṭip pirintatum, tuṣyantaṉ cakuntaliyik kūṭip pirintatum itaṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum.
6. acuram: 1. kollēṟu kōṭal, 2. villēṟṟutal, 3. tiri paṉṟi eytal pōṉṟaṉa ceytu peṇ koḷḷal.
1. itu valiya kāḷiyi aṭakkutal. nappiṉṉi vaḷartta muraṭṭuk kāḷikaḷi aṭakkik kaṇṇaṉ avaḷi maṇantaṉaṉ eṉkiṉṟatu pākavatam. mullik kaliyil varum ēṟutaḻuval eṉpatu, taṅkātaliyar vaḷartta kāḷikaḷiyaṭakkum caṭaṅku pōṉṟa oruvakik kikkiḷiyēyām. 2. tacaratarāmaṉ cītiyiyum, arccuṉaṉ turōpatiyiyum villēṟṟi maṇantaṉar. 3. mūṉṟu paṉṟikaḷi orē ampāl eytal. kāṭṭup paṉṟiyik koṉṟu koṭuttu maṇakkum vaḻakkam iṉṟum aḵcāmiyariṭi yuḷḷatām. arccuṉaṉ mīṉ poṟi vīḻttiyatu tiripaṉṟi yeytalō ṭokkum.
7. irākkatam: peṇ virumpāmalum, peṟṟōr koṭāmalum valitiṟkoḷvatu. vīṭumaṉ ampi mutaliya peṇkaḷi valitiṟkoṇṭu vantu taṉ tampiyarkku maṇa muṭittatu itaṉ pāṟpaṭum.
8. picācam: 1. mūttāḷiyum, 2. kaḷḷuṇṭu kaḷittāḷiyum, 3. tuyiṉṟāḷiyum puṇartalum, 4. iḻintāḷi maṇattalum, 5. āṭimāṟṟutalumām. ivaṟṟukkup purāṇaṅkaḷil eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ uṇṭu.
ivvāriya maṇaṅkaḷ eṭṭum, tamiḻk kātal maṇattiṟku māṟupaṭṭavi eṉpati aṟika. tamiḻk kaḷavoḻukkam otta aṉpiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatu; iyaṟkiyō ṭoṉṟiyatu. 	(2)
1. kāṭci
121. 	oṉṟē vēṟē eṉṟiru pālvayiṉ
oṉṟi yuyarnta pāla tāṇiyiṉ
otta kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyuṅ kāṇpa.
mikkō ṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭivari yiṉṟē.
kāṭci, aiyam, teḷital, tēṟal eṉa iyaṟkip puṇarcci nāṉku vakippaṭum.
1. kāṭci: taṉittuḷḷa taliviyit talivaṉ kāṇutal. iruvarumē kāṇpārāyiṉum talivi mikka nāṇuṭimiyāl tāṉākak kuṟippuri nikaḻttāḷākiyāl, kāṇutal talivaṟkē ciṟanta tāyiṟṟu.
2. aiyam: ivaḷum nammēṟ kātal koṇṭaṉaḷō eṉat talivaṉ aiyuṟutal.
3. teḷital: ivaḷ nammēṟ kātal koṇṭuḷḷāḷeṉat tuṇital.
4. tēṟal: aṅṅaṉam teḷinta talivaṉ, kaṇkaḷāl kuṟippuri nikaḻttit taliviyiṉ karutti aṟivaṉ. itu tēṟal eṉappaṭum.
ivi nāṉku perumpālum talivaṉ māṭṭē nikaḻtaliṉ orutalik kāmamākiya kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭumeṉka.
innāṉkaṉuḷ, iccūttiram mutaṟkaṇṇa tākiya kāṭciyiṉ ilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
i - ḷ : oṉṟē vēṟē eṉṟu iru pāl vayiṉ - iruvarkkum ōriṭamum vēṟiṭamum ākiya iru nilattiṉ kaṇṇum,pālatu āṇiyiṉ - aṉpu tōṉṟutaṟkuriya āṇpāl peṇpāl eṉṉum iru kūṟṟiṉ kaṭṭaḷippaṭi, oṉṟi uyarnta - iruvarkkum aṉpu oṟṟumip paṭṭu uyarnta, otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa - piṟappu mutaliya pattum (mey - 25) otta talivaṉum taliviyum etirppaṭuvar, mikkōṉ āyiṉum kaṭivari iṉṟu - talivaṉ ciṟitu āṇṭil mūttavaṉāyiṉum nīkkum nilimiyiṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.
pālatu, atu - pakutip poruḷ vikuti. pāl - tokuti porumi. pālatu āṇiyil kāṇpa eṉak kūṭṭuka. āṇmi peṇmi eṉṉum irupāliṉ tūṇṭutaliṉālēyē aṉpu tōṉṟutaliṉ, attūṇṭutalip ‘pālatu āṇi’ eṉṟār. uyarnta kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti eṉavum, otta kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti eṉavum kūṭṭuka. uyarnta, otta - peyareccaṅkaḷ. kaṭivari - nīkkum nilimi.
allatu, oṉṟi uyarnta pālatu āṇiyiṉ ottakiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa - kuṟiviṉṟi amintu uyarvuṟṟa āṇpāl peṇpāl kūṟṟiṉ kaṭṭaḷiyiṉāl, piṟappu mutaliya otta iruvarum kāṇpar eṉṟumām.
āṇuṟuppum peṇṇuṟuppum kuṟipaṭiṉ āṇpāl peṇpāl ākāmal alippālākumaṉṟē? avvāṟu uṟuppuk kuṟivuṭiya āṇpāli uṟuppu niṟivuṭiya peṇpālō, peṇpāli āṇpālō etirppaṭiṉ, akkuṟiyuṭiyārkku aṉpu tōṉṟātākiyāl, avvirupālum kuṟiviṉṟi niṟivuṟṟa teṉpa taṟivittaṟku, ‘oṉṟi uyarnta pālatāṇiyiṉ’ eṉṟār, itu otta kāmamākiyāl. niṟinta āṇmiyum peṇmiyum uṭiyār iruvar etirppaṭṭa vaḻiyē oruvari yoruvar kātal koḷvareṉṟu, kātal koḷvataṟkēṟṟa iruvar iṉṉa taṉmiyār eṉpatu kūṟiṉār.‘ uruvu niṟutta kāmavāyil’ (mey - 25) eṉpatum ikkaruttē.
muṉ (akat - 16) kuṟiñci nilattiṉ kaṇṇē puṇarcci nikaḻumeṉṟār. talivaṉum taliviyum kuṟiñci nilattiṉarō eṉṉum aiyam nikaḻumaṉṟē? ‘oṉṟē vēṟē eṉṟiru pālvayiṉ’ eṉa avviyattip pōkkiṉār. atāvatu, iruvarum kuṟiñci nilattiṉarāka viruntu atē nilattil etirppaṭalum kūṭum. talivi kuṟiñci nilattiṉaḷum talivaṉ vēṟu nilattiṉaṉākavumiruntu kuṟiñci nilattil etirppaṭalum kūṭum. talivaṉ kuṟiñci nilattiṉaṉum, talivi vēṟu nilattiṉaḷāyu miruntu kuṟiñci nilattil etirppaṭaluṅ kūṭum. iruvarum vēṟu nilattiṉaṉākavu miruntu kuṟiñci nilattil etirppaṭalum kūṭum. mulli mutaliya vēṟu nilaṅkaḷil etippaṭaluṅ kūṭum. enta nilattil etirppaṭiṉum kuṟiñci nilattil etippaṭṭāreṉal pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam. itu, perum pāṉmi paṟṟiya vaḻakku. vēṉiliṟ poḻutupōkkiṭamum kuṟiñciyātalaṟika.
oppumi pattāvaṉa : piṟappum oḻukkamum āṇmiyum āṇṭum uruvamum aṉpum aṭakkamum aruḷum aṟivum celvamum eṉpaṉa (mey - 25). 	(3)
2. aiyam
122. 	ciṟantuḻi yiyañ ciṟanta teṉpa
iḻintuḻi yiḻipē cuṭṭa lāṉa.
i - ḷ: iḻintuḻi iḻipē cuṭṭalāṉa - talivi kāmap paruvam aṭiyā tiruntāl, talivaṉ māṭṭē nikaḻum orutalik kāmamākiya kikkiḷik kuṟippē yāmākaliṉ, ciṟantuḻi aiyam ciṟantatu eṉpa - talimakaḷ kāmap paruvattaḷākiya kāliyē talivaṉ māṭṭu nikaḻum aiyam ciṟantu tōṉṟum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
ciṟappu - kāmapparuvattiyum, iḻipu - apparuva miṉmiyiyum kuṟittatu. iḻipu - iḻinta kāmamākiya kikkiḷi. talivi kāmapparuva maṭiyāḷāyiṉ, talivaṉ aiyanteḷintu, kuṟippuri peṟṟuk kūṭutal kūṭāmiyiṉ, kāmapparuvamuḷḷa talivimāṭṭuk koṇṭa aiyamē ciṟantateṉpatām. itu, aiyattiṉ ciṟappuk kūṟiyatu.
talivi kāmapparuvam (pūppu) aṭiyātirukkiṉ, talivaṉ kuṟippukku etirkkuṟippu nikaḻttāmaṟ celvāḷākiyāl, avaḷ māṭṭut talivaṉ kātal koṇṭu, ivaḷ nammik kātalik kiṉṟaṉaḷō’ eṉa aiyaṅkoḷḷiṉ, atu piṉ payaṉpaṭāmiyāl orutalik kāmamākiya kikkiḷiyē yākum. avaḷ kāmapparuva maṭintiruppiṉ, talivaṉ kuṟippukku etirkkuṟippu nikaḻttip  piṉṉark kūṭuvāḷākiyāl, avaḷ māṭṭut talivaṉ koṇṭa aiyamē piṉ payaṉpaṭutaliṉ ciṟantatākum ākiyāl, ‘ciṟantuḻi aiyam ciṟantatu’ eṉṟār.
aiyamāvatu: evviṭattiṉaḷō, yār makaḷō, paruva muṟṟaṉaḷō, nammītu kātaṟ kuṟippuṭiyaḷō eṉat talivaṉ aiyuṟutal.
kāṭṭu
“yārō ivaḷtāṉ evvūrō,
	ayalō ikkuṉ ṟiṉaḷōtāṉ,
pērē tōyā rōvivaḷip 
	peṟṟōr, paruva muṟṟaṉaḷō,
ōrē ṉāṉē yatuvaṉṟi
	oppoṉ pāṉu muṭiyāḷō,
kārō māṉāk karuṅkuḻalāḷ
	karuttu metuvō aṟiyēṉē!”	(irāvaṇa kāviyam)
eṉak kāṇka.	(4)
3. teḷital
123. 	vaṇṭē iḻiyē vaḷḷi pūvē
kaṇṇē alamara limippē accameṉ
ṟaṉṉavi piṟavum āṅkavaṇ nikaḻa
niṉṟavi kaḷiyuṅ karuvi yeṉpa.
itu, teḷitaṟkaruvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
i - ḷ: vaṇṭu mutaliya eṭṭum teḷitaṟ karuvikaḷākum.
nikaḻa niṉṟavi - aiyam. palavakiyāka aiyuṟuvāṉākaliṉ, ‘niṉṟavi’ eṉap paṉmi kūṟiṉār. āṅku avaṇ - avvetirp paṭṭa iṭattu.
teḷitalāvatu - ivaḷ pakkattu ūriṉaḷ; nammītu kātal kuṟippuṭiyavaḷ; celvakkuṭiyiṉaḷ mutaliyaṉavākat teḷitalām.
1. nāṭōṟum paḻakiyaviṭat tallatu vaṇṭu cellātākaliṉ, malarmāliyiṉkaṇ tātūtum vaṇṭukaḷāṟ pakkattāḷeṉavum; 2. iḻi - aṇikalaṉ. aṇikalaṉkaḷāṟ celvak
kuṭiyiṉaḷ eṉavum; 3. vaḷḷi - toyyiṟkoṭi. atāvatu, cantaṉak kuḻampāl tōḷkaḷiṟ koṭikaḷ pōla eḻututal. paruvamuṟṟa peṇkaḷē tammi mikka aḻaku ceytu koḷ
vārākiyāl, toyyilāl kāmapparuva maṭintāḷeṉavum teḷital. 4. pū - kiyiluḷḷa kaḻunīrppūvik kaṇṇukku oppiṭṭup pārttal; 5. kaṇ - kāmak kuṟippu nikaḻttuṅ kaṇ; 6. alamaral - kāmakkuṟippāluṇṭāṉa cuḻaṟci; 7. imippu - talimakaṉ nōkkuṅkāl nilaṉōkkal; 8. accam - taṉṉi yottavōr āṇmakaṉit taṉiyiṭattiṟ kaṇṭataṉā luṇṭāṉa accam. ivaṟṟāl talimakaḷ taṉmītu kātaṟkuṟippuṇmiyit talimakaṉ teḷivāṉ.
kāṭṭu
	mikkiṉṟa aṇiccumiyāḷ, miñiṟṟuk kāppāḷ,
mēlellāṅ koṭipaṭarntu viḷaṅku kiṉṟāḷ,
ikkuṉṟāl ivvūrāḷ celva mikkāḷ
eṉṉōkkuk ketirnōkku meṉavuṭ koṇṭāṉ. (araciyalaraṅkam)
eṉak kāṇka. miñiṟu - vaṇṭu. 	(5)
4. tēṟal
124. 	nāṭṭa miraṇṭum aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
kūṭṭi yurikkum kuṟippuri yākum.
itu, aṅṅaṉam aiyuṟṟut teḷinta talivaṉ kuṟippāl taṉ kātali veḷiyiṭat taliviyum avvāṟē taṉ kuṟippi veḷiyiṭu meṉkiṉṟatu.
i - ḷ: aṟivu uṭampaṭuttaṟku - taṉ karuttit talivi uṭampaṭumpaṭi ceyvataṟku, nāṭṭam iraṇṭum kūṭṭi urikkum - iru kaṇkaḷālum talivaṉ kuṟippākak kūṟuvāṉ, kuṟippuri ākum - taliviyum avvāṟē taṉṉuṭampāṭṭik kuṟippāṟ kūṟuvāḷ.
talivaṉ kaṇkaḷāl taṉ kuṟippi yuṇarttuvāṉ; taliviyum avvāṟē taṉ kuṟippi yuṇarttuvāḷ eṉpatām. ‘nāṭṭam iraṇṭum’ eṉpati, iraṇṭiṭattum kūṭṭuka. nāṭutal - nāṭṭam, toḻiṟpeyar. nāṭṭam - kaṇ.
kāṭṭu
“	nōkkiṉāḷ nōkki yiṟiñciṉā ḷaḵtavaḷ
yāppiṉu ḷaṭṭiya nīr.”				 (kuṟaḷ)
eṉak kāṇka. 	(6)
5. talivi kuṟippuri
125. 	taṉṉuṟu vēṭki kiḻavaṉmuṟ kiḷattal
eṇṇuṅ kālik kiḻattik killip
piṟanīr mākkaḷi ṉaṟiya vāyiṭip
peynīr pōlum uṇarviṟ ṟeṉpa.
i - ḷ : piṟanīr mākkaḷiṉ - otta aṉpillāta ovvāk kātalar pōla, kiḻavaṉ aṟiyat taṉṉuṟu vēṭki muṉ kiḷattal kiḻattikku illi - talivaṉ aṟiyumpaṭi taṉatu kātaṟ perukki attalivaṉ muṉ talivi kūṟāḷ, āyiṭi - avaḷ kūṟātu niṉṟaviṭattum, eṇṇum kāli - taliviyiṉ vēṭkiyit talivaṉ ārāyumpoḻutu, peynīr pōlum uṇarviṟṟu eṉpa - taliviyiṉ vēṭkiyāṉatu, putumaṭkalatti lūṟṟiya nīr puṟattup pocintu kāṭṭuvatupōlum uṇarviṉi yuṭittu eṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉṟavāṟu.
mākkaḷiṉ, iṉ - oppupporuḷ. veḷippaṭak kūṟāta tōṭu, kuṟippiṉāluṅ kūṟātu, kuṟittuṇarumpaṭi kāṭṭuvaḷ eṉpatām. atāvatu, talivi taṉmītu kātaluḷḷāḷ eṉpati, avaḷ uṭaṟ kuṟippāl talivaṉ aṟintu koḷḷumpaṭi ceytal. ‘kiḻavaṉ muṉ kiḷattal illi’ eṉavē, taṉatu vēṭkiyit talivi tōḻikku etirniṉṟu kūṟuvaḷ.
kāṭṭu
       “	kaṭumpuṉal malinta kāvirip pēriyāṟṟu
neṭuñcuḻi nīttam maṇṇunaḷ pōla
naṭuṅkañar petira muyaṅki nerunal
āka maṭitan tōḷē.”				  (kam - 62)
itu, talivi vēṭkiyit talivaṉ kuṟippāluṇarntami kūṟiyatu. nīrāṭuvāḷ pōlak kikaḷi virittu muyaṅkaṟ kuṟip puṇarttiṉāḷeṉak kūṟiṉami kāṇka. 	(7)
6. kūṭal viruppam
126. 	kuṟippē kuṟittatu koḷḷu māyiṉ
āṅkavi nikaḻu meṉmaṉār pulavar.
i - ḷ : kuṟittatu - talivaṉ kuṟitta puṇarcci vēṭkiyē, kuṟippuk koḷḷumāyiṉ - taliviyuṅ karutuvāḷāṉāl, āṅku - appōtu, avi nikaḻum eṉmaṉār pulavar - pukumukam purital mutaliya meyppāṭukaḷ nāṉkum talivi māṭṭu nikaḻum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
āṅku - talivaṉ kuṟippiyē taliviyuṅ koṇṭa kālattu eṉka.
mutalmeyppāṭu nāṉku
1. pukumukam purital	3. nakunaya maṟittal
2. poṟinutal viyarttal	4. citivu piṟarkkiṉmi
eṉpaṉa. ivi, maṭami eṉṉum peṇmik kuṇattiṉāl ēṟpaṭuvaṉa. (mey - 13 uri pārkka). eṭuttukkāṭṭu āṅkuk kāṇka.
kāṭci, aiyam, teḷital, tēṟal, kūṭal viruppam eṉpavi meyyuṟu puṇarccikku ētuvākum. avvāṟu meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiṉṟi, uḷḷappuṇarcciyē nikaḻntu maṇantu koḷḷum kātalarkaḷum uṇṭu. atu mēṟkūṟupa. uḷḷap puṇarcci - oruvariyoruvar maṉattiṭattē aṉpukoḷḷutal. 	(8)
7. uḷḷap puṇarcci
1. āṇṭaṉmi
127.  	perumiyum uraṉum āṭūu mēṉa.
i - ḷ: perumiyum valiyum āṇmit taṉmiyākum.
1. perumiyāvatu - aṟivu, āṟṟal, pukaḻ, koṭi, ārāycci, paṇpu, naṇpu, paḻipāvaṅkaṭ kañcutal mutaliyavaṟṟil mēmpaṭutal.
2. valiyāvatu - kaṭippiṭi, aṭakkam, tuṇivu mutaliyavi. kaṭippiṭi - koṇṭati viṭāmaṟ ceytu muṭittal. aṟivāṟṟalkaḷālum, ārāycci accaṅkaḷālum, aṭakkam tuṇivu ivaṟṟālum talivaṉ mēmpaṭṭu uḷḷap puṇarcciyē nikaḻntu varintu koḷḷutaluṅ kūṭum eṉpatām. varital - maṇattal.      (9)
2. peṇṭaṉmi
128. 	accamu nāṇu maṭaṉumun tuṟutta
niccamum peṇpāṟ kuriya veṉpa.
i - ḷ: accamum - aṉpu kāraṇamākat tōṉṟiya accamum, nāṇum - kāmakkuṟippu nikaḻnta vaḻi uṇṭākum nāṇamum, maṭaṉum - aṟivikka aṟintu aṟintati veḷikkuk kāṭṭāmiyākiya maṭaṉum, muntuṟutta - ākiya immūṉṟiyum mutalākavuṭiya kuṇaṅkaḷ, niccamum - ennāḷum, peṇpāṟku uriya eṉpa - peṇkaḷukku uriyaveṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉṟavāṟu.
‘muntuṟutta’ eṉṟataṉāl, payirppu, pētimi, niṟi eṉṉum kuṇaṅkaḷuṅ koḷka. payirppu - putitāka oṉṟik kaṇṭa viṭattu maṉaṅkoḷḷāmi. pētimi - kāma
vēṭkiyāl ceyyat takuvaṉa aṟiyāmi. niṟi - aṭakkam. ‘niṟiyeṉap paṭuvatu maṟipiṟa raṟiyāmi.’ maṟi - kaḷavu.
accam, nāṇam, maṭam, payirppu, pētimi, niṟi eṉṉum kuṇaṅkaḷāl talimakaḷ uḷḷap puṇarcciyē puṇarntu, meyyuṟu puṇarcciyi virumpāmal maṇantu koḷḷumeṉka. ip peṇmik kuṇaṅkaḷ āṟaṉuḷ, pētimi nāṇilum, niṟi accattilum aṭaṅkutalāṉ, nāṇam, maṭam, accam, payirppu eṉak kuṇam nāṉ keṉṟalum uṇṭu. ‘nāṟkuṇamum nāṟpaṭiyā’ (naḷa - 32). 	(10)
1. talivaṉ kūṟṟu
129.	meytoṭṭup payiṟal, poypā rāṭṭal,
iṭam peṟṟut taḻāal, iṭiyūṟu kiḷattal,
nīṭuniṉin tiraṅkal, kūṭuta luṟutal,
colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻit
tīrāt tēṟṟam uḷappaṭat tokiip
pērāc ciṟappiṉ irunāṉku kiḷaviyum;
peṟṟavaḻi makiḻcciyum, pirintavaḻik kalaṅkalum,
niṟpavi niṉii nikaḻpavi urippiṉum, 
kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭpiṉum,
peṭṭa vāyilpeṟ ṟiravuvali yuṟuppiṉum,
ūrum pērum keṭutiyum piṟavum
nīriṟ kuṟippiṉ nirampak kūṟit
tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟum pakutiyum, tōḻi
kuṟiyavaṭ cārtti meyyuṟuk kūṟalum,
taṇṭā tirappiṉum, maṟṟiya vaḻiyum,
collavaṭ cārttaliṟ pulliya vakiyiṉum,
aṟintōḷ ayirppiṉ avvaḻi maruṅkiṟ
kēṭum pīṭum kūṟalum, tōḻi
nīkkali ṉākiya nilimiyum, nōkki
maṭaṉmāk kūṟum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē.
ikkūṟṟukkaḷ irupattu nāṉkaṉuḷ, 1 - 8 iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalip pāṭṭiṟkum, 9 - 13 pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭat tiṟkum, 14 - 17 um, 18 - 24 um iruvaki matiyuṭam pāṭṭiṟkum uriyaṉa vākum. 	(11)
2. ituvumatu
130. 	paṇpiṟ peyarppiṉum parivuṟṟu meliyiṉum
aṉpuṟṟu nakiṉum avaṭpeṟṟu maliyiṉum
āṟṟiṭi yuṟutalum avviṉik kiyalpē.
ikkūṟṟukkaḷ aintaṉuḷ, oṉṟu matiyuṭampāṭu iraṇṭāvatu kūṭṭattiṟkum, 2 - 5 kuṟiyiṭattiṟkum uriyaṉavām. 		(12)
3. tōḻi kūṟṟu
131. 	nāṟṟamun tōṟṟamum oḻukkamum uṇṭiyum 
ceyviṉi maṟippiṉum celaviṉum payilviṉum
puṇarcci yetirppā ṭuḷḷuṟuttu varūum
uṇarcci yēḻiṉum uṇarnta piṉṟi
meyyiṉum poyyiṉum vaḻinili piḻiyātu
palvēṟu kavarporuḷ nāṭṭat tāṉum,
kuṟiyuṟaṟ ketiriya kiḻavaṉi maṟiyuṟap
perumiyiṟ peyarppiṉu mulakurit toḻippiṉum,
arumiyi ṉakaṟciyum avaḷaṟi vuṟuttup
piṉvaru keṉṟalum pētimi yūṭṭalum
muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟiniṟut turittalum
añciyac cuṟuttalum urittuḻik kūṭṭamō
ṭeñcātu kiḷanta irunāṉku kiḷaviyum,
vanta kiḻavaṉi māyañ ceppip
poṟutta kāraṇaṅ kuṟitta kāliyum 
puṇarntapiṉ avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkaṟ kaṇṇum
kuṟintavaṭ paṭariṉum maṟintava ḷarukat
taṉṉoṭu mavaḷoṭum mutaṉmūṉ ṟaḷiip
piṉṉili nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅkiṉum
naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri yiṭattiṉum
eṇṇarum paṉṉaki kaṇṇiya vakiyiṉum
puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum vēṇṭāp piriviṉum
vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭiya viṭattum
puṇarntuḻi yuṇarnta aṟimaṭac ciṟappiṉum 
ōmpaṭik kiḷavip pāṅkiṉ kaṇṇum
ceṅkaṭu moḻiyāṟ citivuṭit tāyiṉum
eṉpunekap pirintōḷ vaḻicceṉṟu kaṭii
aṉputali yaṭutta vaṉpuṟik kaṇṇum
āṟṟatu tīmi aṟivuṟu kalakkamum
kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa variṉum
kaḷaṉum poḻutum varinili vilakkik
kātaṉ mikuti yuḷappaṭap piṟavum
nāṭum ūrum illuṅ kuṭiyum
piṟappuñ ciṟappum iṟappa nōkki
avaṉvayiṟ ṟōṉṟiya kiḷaviyoṭu tokii
aṉanili vakiyāṉ varital vēṇṭiṉum
aiyac ceyki tāykketir maṟuttup
poyyeṉa māṟṟi meyvaḻik koṭuppiṉum
avaṉvilaṅ kuṟiṉum kaḷampeṟak kāṭṭiṉum 
piṟaṉvari vāyiṉu mavaṉvarivu maṟuppiṉum
muṉṉili yaṟaṉeṉap paṭutaleṉ ṟiruvakip
puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuppiṉum
varivuṭaṉ paṭṭōṟ kaṭāva vēṇṭiṉum
āṅkataṉ ṟaṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi yuḷappaṭap
pāṅkuṟa vanta nāleṭṭu vakiyum
tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṟ ṟōḻi mēṉa.
ikkūṟṟukkaḷ nāṟpataṉuḷ, 1 - 8 matiyuṭampāṭu mutaṟ kūṭṭat tiṟkum, 9 - 16 atilēyē aṭuttum, 17 - 20 matiyuṭam pāṭu iraṇṭāvatu kūṭṭattiṟkum, 21 -31 kuṟiyiṭattiṟkum, 32 - 37 varivu kaṭātaṟkum, 38 - 40 aṟattoṭu nilikkum uriyavākum.	(13)
4. talivi kūṟṟu
132.	iruvakik kuṟipiḻip pākiya viṭattum
kāṇā vakiyiṟ poḻutunaṉi yikappiṉum
tāṉakam pukāaṉ peyarta liṉmiyiṟ
kāṭci yāciyiṟ kaḷampukkuk kalaṅki
vēṭkiyiṉ mayaṅkiya kiyaṟu poḻutiṉum
pukāak kālip pukketirp paṭṭuḻip
pakāa viruntiṉ pakutik kaṇṇum
vēḷā ṇetirum viruntiṉ kaṇṇum
vāḷā ṉetirum pirivi ṉāṉum
nāṇuneñ calippa viṭuttaṟ kaṇṇum
varital vēṇṭit tōḻi ceppiya
puritīr kiḷavi pulliya etirum
varivuṭaṉ paṭutalum āṅkataṉ puṟattup
puripaṭa vanta maṟuttaloṭu tokiik
kiḻavōḷ mēṉa veṉmaṉār pulavar.
ik kūṟṟukaḷ pattaṉuḷ, 1 - 5 kuṟiyiṭattiṟkum, 6 - 10 varivu kaṭātaṟkum uriyavākum.	(14)
5. cevili kūṟṟu
133.	kaḷavala rāyiṉum kāmameyp paṭuppiṉum
aḷavu mikat tōṉṟiṉum talippeytu kāṇiṉum
kaṭṭiṉuṅ kaḻaṅkiṉum veṟiyeṉa iruvarum
oṭṭiya tiṟattāṟ ceytik kaṇṇum
āṭiya ceṉṟuḻi yaṟivutali variṉum
kātal kimmikak kaṉavi ṉaraṟṟalum
tōḻiyi viṉavalun teyvam vāḻttalum
pōkkuṭa ṉaṟintapiṉ tōḻiyoṭu keḻīik
kaṟpi ṉākkattu niṟṟaṟ kaṇṇum
pirivi ṉeccattu makaṇeñcu valippiṉum
irupāṟ kuṭipporu ḷiyalpiṉ kaṇṇum
iṉṉa vakiyiṟ patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyō
ṭaṉṉavi piṟavuñ cevili mēṉa.		        (15)
6. naṟṟāy kūṟṟu
134. 	tāykkum variyār pōkkuṭaṉ kiḷappiṉ.
ik kūṟṟukaḷ patiṉmūṉṟuḷ, 1 - 4 kuṟiyiṭattiṟkum, 5- 9 varivu kaṭātaṟkum, 10 - 13 uṭaṉ pōkkiṟkum uriyavākum.
kuṟippu: ivvintu cūttirattum palaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟum talivaṉ mutaliyōr kūṟṟukkaḷit tokuttuk kūṟuvatāl, aṅkaṅkē eṭuttāḷutaṟ poruṭṭum, eḷitil viḷaṅkutaṟ poruṭṭum iṅku oru cēra eḻutappaṭṭaṉa.
1. talivaṉ ceyal 11:  1 - 8
1. 	meytoṭṭup payiṟal	5. 	nīṭuniṉin tiraṅkal
2. 	poy pārāṭṭal	6. 	kūṭutaluṟutal
3. 	iṭam peṟṟut taḻāl	7. 	colliya nukarcci vallē 					peṟutal
4. 	iṭiyūṟu kiḷattal	8. 	tīrāt tēṟṟam
itaṉ poruḷ:
1. meytoṭṭup payiṟal - talivaṉ kuṟippuriyi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa talivipāl  ‘pukumukam purital’ mutaliya nāṉku meyp pāṭukaḷum nikaḻva tuṇarnta talivaṉ, taliviyiṉ puṇarccikkuṟip paṟiya vēṇṭit talivi yuṭampiṉit toṭuvāṉ. payiṟal - kuṟip paṟital; appayiṟciyik kuṟippiṭuvāṉ.
2. poy pārāṭṭal - aṅṅaṉan toṭṭuk kuṟippaṟintavaṉ, taliviyiṉ kūntaliyum neṟṟiyiyum nīvi, ‘kūntal kalintatu, atit tiruttukiṟēṉ; neṟṟi viyarttatu; atit tuṭikkiṟēṉ’ eṉak kūṟuvāṉ. poyyi meyyākak kūṟutaleṉka.
ivvāṟu meyyit toṭavum, payirppu eṉṉum peṇmik kuṇattāl, talivipāl,
5. kūḻi virittal
6. kātoṉṟu kaḷital
7. ūḻaṇi tivaral
8. uṭi peyarttuṭuttal
eṉum iraṇṭāvatu meyppāṭu nāṉkum nikaḻum. (mey - 14)......
3. iṭam peṟṟut taḻāl - meytoṭṭu, poy pārāṭṭiya vaḻit talivi talivaṉ mukattip pārppāḷ. talivaṉ appārviyic ciṟappittuk kūṟuvaṉ. iṭam - talivi pārvi. taḻāl - taḻuvutal. atāvatu taliviyiṉ pārviyit talivaṉ peṟṟut taṉatākkik koḷḷutal. ‘ummavar yāṉiyi ōrampāl eytu pōkkuvar; nī nāṉ pōkāmal īrampāl eytaṉi’ eṉak kāṇka.
4. iṭiyūṟu kiḷattal - talivi peru nāṇiṉaḷātaliṉ, aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟak kēṭṭuk kaṇkaḷi mūṭik koṇṭum, ceṭi maṟiviṟ pōy niṉṟu koṇṭum talivaṉip pārāmal iṭiyūṟu ceyvāḷ; talivaṉ atik kūṟuvāṉ.
5. nīṭu niṉintu iraṅkal - aṅṅaṉam kūṟiyum talivi iṇaṅkāmiyiṉāl, nīṇṭa nēram āṉatu eṉat talivaṉ irakkan tōṉṟak kūṟuvaṉ. nēram nīṇṭati eṇṇi iraṅkuvaṉ eṉka.
6. kūṭutal uṟutal - atukēṭṭa talivi, talivaṉatu āṟṟāmik kiraṅkip perunāṇ virivil nīṅkuvāḷ, atiyum talivaṉ kūṟuvaṉ. kūṭutaṟku iṇaṅkutal.
iṭam peṟṟut taḻāl toṭaṅkiyatum talivi māṭṭu,
9. alkul tivaral
10. aṇintavi tiruttal
11. ilvali yuṟuttal
12. irukiyu meṭuttal
eṉṉum mūṉṟāvatu meyppāṭu nāṉkum nikaḻum (mey - 15) ‘irukiyu meṭuttal’ kūṭutaluṟutaliṉ piṉṉar nikaḻum.
7. colliya nukarcci vallē peṟutal - talivaṉtāṉ muṟkūṟiya puṇarcciyi viriviṟ peṟutal. nukarcci - puṇarcci.
8. tīrāt tēṟṟam - aṅṅaṉam kūṭiyavaṉ, ‘iṉi eṉṟum uṉṉip piriyēṉ’ eṉac cūḷurippaṉ. tēṟṟam - teḷivu. tīrāt tēṟṟam - cūḷāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam puṇarcci nikaḻnta piṉ talivimāṭṭu,
13. pārāṭṭeṭuttal (mey - 16) eṉṉum meyppāṭu nikaḻum.
uḷappaṭat tokii - tīrāt tēṟṟam akappaṭat tokuttu, pērāc ciṟappiṉ iru nāṉku kiḷaviyum - kuṟiyāta ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya ivveṭṭum iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉṟavāṟu.
kāṭṭu
1. 	 ‘uṟutō ṟuyirtaḷirppat tīṇṭalāṟ pētik
  kamiḻti ṉiyaṉṟaṉa tōḷ.’			     (kuṟaḷ)
itu, mey toṭṭup payiṟal.
2. 	“ kaṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻvarniṉ ṉiyar
   uṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻvimaṉ nīyum,
   miṭalpuk kaṭaṅkāta ventuṇiyō pāram
  iṭarpuk kiṭukum iṭiyiḻival kaṇṭāy.” 	(cilap - 7)
itu, poypārāṭṭal, mārpiṉ pārantāṅkātu iṭi muriyavuṅ kūṭumeṉa iṭiyit toṭal.
3. 	“kolyāṉi veṇmaruppuṅ kōḷval puliyataḷum
  nalyāṇar niṉṉiyar kūṭṭuṇṭu - celvārtām
  ōrampi ṉāṉeytu pōkvaryāṉ pōkāmi
  īrampi ṉāleytā yiṉṟu.” 		         (tiṇi - nūṟ)
itu, iṭam peṟṟut taḻāl.
4. 	“colliṟ colletir koḷḷāy yāḻaniṉ
  tirumuka miṟiñci nāṇuti katumeṉa.” 		   (naṟ - 39)
itu, iṭiyūṟu kiḷattal
5. 	“kāmaṅ kimmikiṟ ṟāṅkuta leḷitō.”	 (naṟ - 39)
itu, nīṭuniṉin tiraṅkal.
6. 	“ilaṅkuvaḷi teḷirppa alava ṉāṭṭi
  mukamputi katuppiṉa ḷiṟiñciniṉ ṟāḷē
  pulampukoḷ māli maṟiya
  nalaṅkē ḷākam nalkuva ḷeṉakkē.” 	               (aiṅ - 197)
itu, kūṭutaluṟutal.
7. 	“vēṭṭa poḻuti ṉaviyavi pōlumē
  tōṭṭār katuppiṉāḷ tōḷ.” 			      (kuṟaḷ)
itu, nukarcci peṟṟatu.
8. 	“emmaṇaṅ kiṉavē makiḻna,. . . .
  ekkar naṇṇiya emmūr viyaṉṟuṟi
  nēriṟi muṉki paṟṟic
  cūrara makaḷirō ṭuṟṟa cūḷē.” 		             (kuṟun - 53)
itu, tīrāt tēṟṟattip piṉṉorukāl talivi kūṟiyatu. ‘cūḷ em aṇaṅkiṉa’ eṉka.
2. iṭantalippāṭu
iṭantalip pāṭu - muṉ taliviyik kūṭiya atē iṭattu maṟunāḷum talivaṉ ceṉṟu taliviyik kūṭutal. iṭam - atē iṭattil. talippāṭu - talippaṭutal, kūṭutal - cērtal.
muṟkūṟiya meytoṭṭup payiṟal mutaliya eṭṭumē iṭantalip pāṭṭiṟkum uriya.
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyil iv veṭṭum nikaḻntaṉa vēṉum, putitāka etirppaṭṭa tākiyāl maṟunāḷum avveṭṭum nikaḻumeṉka.
tāmē kūṭum iṭantalip pāṭum, pāṅkaṉāl taliviyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaṇṭu vantu kūṟac ceṉṟu kūṭum iṭantalip pāṭum eṉa iṭantalippāṭu iruvakippaṭum. iraṇṭāvatu mikac ciṟupāṉmi.
vaki
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉum, iṭantalip pāṭṭiṉum avveṭṭiṉ vakiyāka varuṅ kiḷavikaḷ.
1. acca mikal, 2. kātal mikal
3. iṉṉum puṇarcci kūṭuṅkol leṉal
4. taliviyi etirppaṭṭa iṭatti avaḷākak kūṟal 
5. kiḷḷi  vāḻttu
6. talivi taṉitta nilimi kaṇṭu viyattal
7. muṉṉart tāṉ nīṅkiyavaḻip piṟanta varuttaṅkūṟal.
8. aṇiyaṇintu viṭuttal - eṉpaṉa.
1. talivaṉ varuvāṉkol eṉat talivikku accamikutal. 2. talivaṉ vārāṉ kol eṉat talivikkuk kātal mikutal. 
5. taliviyuḷḷa puṉattiṭattip pāṭik kāṭṭiya kiḷiyi vāḻttutal.	(16)
iyaṟkip puṇarccip piṉ
talivaṉ piriyum pōtu kūṟuvaṉa
135. 	muṉṉili yākkal colvaḻip paṭuttal
naṉṉaya murittal nakinaṉi yuṟāa
tannili yaṟital melivuviḷak kuṟuttal
tannili yurittal teḷivakap paṭuttaleṉ
ṟaṉṉavi nikaḻum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
1. muṉṉili yākkal
2. colvaḻip paṭuttal
3. naṉṉayam urittal
4. nakinaṉi uṟātu annili aṟital
5. melivu viḷakkuṟuttal
6. tannili yurittal
7. teḷivakap paṭuttal
1, 2, 3 - nayappu. ivi pirital nimittam; taliviyiṉ aiyam nīkkutal. 4. pirivaccam. 5. vaṉpuṟi - vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutal. 6, 7 - tēṟṟa murittal. tēṟṟam - teḷivu.
i - ḷ: 1. muṉṉili ākkal - talivaṉ, muṉṉiliyākāta vaṇṭu, neñcu mutaliyavaṟṟi muṉṉili yākkik kūṟutal.
2. colvaḻip paṭuttal - accollāta vaṇṭu  mutaliyavaṟṟit tāṉ kēṭṭataṟku viṭi kūṟuvaṉapōl kūṟutal.
3. nal nayam urittal - vaṇṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut taṉ kātal viruppatti urittal.
4. naki naṉi uṟātu annili aṟital - aṅṅaṉam taṉṉayap puritta talivaṉ, talivi mikka makiḻcci yaṭiyāta puṇarccikkiṉamāṉa pirivu nili kūṟi, avaḷ āṟṟun taṉmi yaṟital. naki - makiḻcci. nakiyuṟāta nili - pirivu.
5. melivu viḷakkuṟuttal - talivaṉ pirivāṉeṉṟu eṇṇi varuntum talivikku, avaḷuṭiya melivi nīkkutaṟ poruṭṭut talivaṉ taṉṉuṭiya aṉpu niliyāṉateṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttal. atāvatu, iṭamaṇit teṉṟal - eṉ ūr aṇittāka uḷatu; neṭitu pirintu celvēṉeṉa varuntātē eṉak kūṟutal. melivu - varuttam. viḷakkuṟuttal - piriyēṉ eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇatti viḷakkik kāṭṭutal.
6. tannili urittar - taliviyip pirintiruttal taṉakku iyalāteṉṟu kūṟutal.
7. teḷivu akappaṭuttal - ‘uṉṉip piriyēṉ; piriyiṉ āṟṟēṉ; piriyiṉ koṭiyaṉāvēṉ’ eṉat talivi teḷiyumāṟu kūṟutal. akappaṭuttal - teḷivu taṉ coṟkuḷ aṭaṅkumpaṭi kūṟutal.
eṉṟu iṉṉavi nikaḻum eṉmāṉār pulavar - eṉṟu ivi talivaṉ māṭṭu nikaḻumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
kāṭṭu:
      “	koṅkutēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpi,
kāmañ ceppātu kaṇṭatu moḻimō
payiliyatu keḻīiya naṭpiṉ mayiliyaṟ
ceṟiyeyiṟ ṟarivi kūntaliṉ 
naṟiyavu muḷavō nīyaṟiyum pūvē.”			(kuṟun - 2)
itaṉuḷ, ‘tumpi’ eṉṟatu, 1. muṉṉili yākkal. ‘kaṇṭatu moḻimō’ eṉṟatu, 2. colvaḻip paṭuttal. ‘kūntaliṉ naṟiyavum uḷavō’ eṉṟatu, 3. naṉṉaya murittal ‘kāmañ ceppātu’ eṉṟatu, iṭamaṇitteṉa, 5. melivu viḷak kuṟuttatu. ‘payiliyatu keḻīiya naṭpu’ eṉṟatu, 6. tannili yurittal.
      “	nīyē, añca leṉṟaveṉ collañ caliyē
yāṉē, kuṟuṅkā laṉṉam kulavumaṇaṟ cēkkum
kaṭalcūḻ maṇṭilam peṟiṉum
viṭalcū ḻalaṉyāṉ niṉṉuṭi naṭpē.”	           (kuṟun - 300)
itu, nayappum, pirivaccamum, vaṉpuṟiyuṅ kūṟiṟṟu.
‘nī eṉ col añcali’ eṉṟatu - pirital nimittamum, pirivaccamum. ‘niṉṉuṭi naṭpu peṟiṉum viṭal cūḻalaṉ’ eṉṟatāl vaṉpuṟiyum ātal kāṇka.
      “	melliya lariviniṉ nallakam pulampa
niṟṟuṟan tamikuve ṉāyiṉ eṟṟuṟan
tiravalar vārā vikal
palavā kukayāṉ celavuṟu takavē.”		           (kuṟun - 137)
itu, teḷivakappaṭuttal.
vaki
1. āyattuyttal
2. yāṉ pōvaleṉṟal
3. maṟintu avaṭkāṇṭal
4. kaṇṭu niṉṟu avaḷ nili kūṟal
5. avaḷarumi aṟintu kūṟal - eṉpaṉa.
āyam - viḷiyāṭṭu makaḷir, tōḻiyar. avarkaḷiṭam taliviyip pōka viṭutal. 5. āyattuṭaṉ kūṭi viḷiyāṭun taliviyit taṉittu etirppaṭal arumi eṉpati aṟintu taṉakkuḷ kūṟutal.
itaṉ piṉṉarum, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻnta piṉṉarum talivimāṭṭu,
14. maṭantapa vurittal 
15. īramil kūṟṟam ēṟṟalar nāṇal
16. koṭuppavi kōṭal	(mey - 16)
eṉṉum nālāvatu meyppāṭu mūṉṟum nikaḻum. koṭuppavi kōṭal pāṅki matiyuṭampaṭṭa piṉṉarum nikaḻum.	(17)
iyaṟkippuṇarcci mutal kaḷavu veḷippaṭu maḷavum iruvarkkum uḷavām ilakkaṇam
136. 	vēṭki yorutali yuḷḷutal melital 
ākkañ ceppal nāṇuvari yiṟattal
nōkkuva vellām aviyē pōṟal
maṟattal mayakkam cākkā ṭeṉṟac
ciṟappuṭi marapiṉavi kaḷaveṉa moḻipa.
1. 	orutali vēṭki 	6. 	nōkkiya vellām 					aviyē pōṟal
2. 	orutali yuḷḷutal 	7. 	maṟattal
3. 	melital 	8. 	mayakkam
4. 	ākkañ ceppal 	9. 	cākkāṭu.
5. 	nāṇuvari yiṟattal
i-ḷ: 1. orutali vēṭki - iruvarkkum orutaliyāka vēṭki nikaḻum. 
2. orutali uḷḷutal - iṭiviṭātu oruvari yoruvar niṉippar. orutali  - tuṇivu. ‘orutali’ eṉpati īriṭattum kūṭṭuka. 
3. melital - ataṉāl uṭampu melivar.
4. ākkam ceppal - ētāvatu ōr iṭiyūṟu kēṭṭa vaḻi ataṉi naṉmiyāka neñciṟkuk kūṟikkoḷvar. ākkam - āvatu. naṉmi. tōḻi cēṭpaṭuttavaḻi, talivaṉ ataṉi aṉpeṉṟu koḷḷutalum, iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarntu nīṅkiya vaḻit talivi ataṉi aṉpeṉṟu koḷḷutalum pōlvaṉa ākkañ ceppalākum. cēṭpaṭuttal - taliviyi aṇukaviṭātu talivaṉi nīkki niṟuttal.
5. nāṇuvari iṟattal - iyaṉṟa maṭṭilum nāṇi, kātal mikunta vaḻi nāṇi viṭal iruvarkkum iyalpē. vari - elli. iṟattal - kaṭattal. itaṉāl, paṇṭu āṭavarum nāṇuṭiyarāy iruntami peṟappaṭum. talivaṉ pāṅkaṟkum tōḻikkum urittalum, talivi tōḻikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟalum pōlvaṉa - nāṇuvari iṟattalām.
6. nōkkuva ellām aviyē pōṟal - piṟar tammi nōkkiya nōkkamellām tammaṉattu maṟittu oḻukukiṉṟa kaḷavoḻukkatti aṟintu nōkkukiṟār eṉak koḷvar. nōkkutal - pārttal.
7. maṟattal - piṟa toḻilkaḷi maṟantu naṭappar. piṟa - viḷiyāṭal mutaliyaṉa.
8. mayakkam - ceyvaṉa iṉṉa veṉṟu aṟiyāmal mayaṅkutal. atāvatu, puḷḷoṭum vilaṅkoṭum kūṟutal.
9. cākkāṭu - cāviṟ kētuvāṉavaṟṟik kūṟutal. talivaṉ maṭalēṟuvaṉ, varipāyvaṉ eṉak kūṟuvaṉ. talivi kātal mikutiyāl uṭal melintu varuntik kūṟuvaḷ. atāvatu, nalaṉaḻivu kūṟalām. nalaṉ - aḻaku. ataṉ mēlum iṟceṟippāl talivaṉik kūṭap peṟāviṭiṉ kātal nōyāl iṟantupaṭutaluṅ kūṭum. iṉṟum taṅkātalari yaṭiyap peṟāta cila peṇkaḷ taṟkoli ceytu koḷḷutali aṟika. ‘kātal tappiṉ cātal’ eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. orutali vēṭki mutaliya oṉpatum kaḷaviṟ kaṉṟik kaṟpiṟ kākā. eṉṟu acciṟappuṭi marapiṉavi kaḷavu eṉa moḻipa - eṉṟu collappaṭṭa antac ciṟappuṭit tāṉa muṟiyiṉi yuṭiya oṉpatum kaḷavoḻukkameṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉṟavāṟu.
kāṭṭu
       “	aṇiṟpal laṉṉa koṅkumutir muṇṭakattu
maṇikkē ḻaṉṉa mānīrc cērppa!
immi māṟi maṟumi yāyiṉum
nīyā kiyareṅ kaṇavaṉi
yāṉā kiyarniṉ neñcunēr pavaḷē.”	              (kuṟun - 49)
itu, cākkāṭu. ‘immi māṟi’ eṉavē, piriyiṉ iṟantu paṭuvēṉ eṉal kāṇka.
3. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam - 1
itu, 1. talivaṉ pāṅkaṉic ceṉṟu kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam, 2. pāṅkaṉāl taliviyik kūṭuṅkūṭṭam eṉa iruvakip paṭum.
talivaṉ kūṟṟu 11: 9 - 3
9. peṟṟa vaḻi makiḻcci 		12. nikaḻpavi urittal
10. pirinta vaḻik kalaṅkal 	13. kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭiya vāyil 				              peṭṭal
11. niṟpavi niṉii urittal
i - ḷ: 9. peṟṟa vaḻi makiḻcciyum - kūṭiya piṉṉark kūṭṭattiṉ nayattit talivaṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟi makiḻvāṉ.
10. pirinta vaḻik kalaṅkalum - pirintu ceṉṟa taliviyit talivaṉ maṟintu niṉṟu āyattiṭik kaṇṭu, iṉikkūṭutal ariteṉṟu kalakka muṟuvaṉ. talivi maṟintu niṉṟu talivaṉik kāṇṭalum uṇṭu. peṟṟa vaḻi makiḻcciyum, pirinta vaḻik kalaṅkalum talivikkum uṇṭu.
ivviraṇṭum iṭantalip pāṭṭiṟkum pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṟkum iṭiyē nikaḻum. itaṉ piṉṉart talivaṉ pāṅkaṉi aṭiyavē, avaṉ, ‘ivvaḷavu nēram eṅku ceṉṟīr?’ eṉa viṉāvutalum, ataṟkut talivaṉ taṉṉi orutti varuttiyatākak kūṟutalum nikaḻum.
11. niṟpavi niṉii urippiṉum - atu kēṭṭa pāṅkaṉ, ulakattu niliniṟkiṉṟa nalloḻukkatti niṉippittuk kūṟuvāṉ; atāvatu, niṉpōṉṟa talivaṉukku immaṟinta oḻukkam takātu eṉak kūṟutal.
12. nikaḻpavi urippiṉum - atu kēṭṭa talivaṉ, ati maṟuttut taṉ maṉattiṉkaṇ nikaḻum varuttaṅkaḷik kūṟuvāṉ, ‘urippiṉum’ eṉpati iraṇṭiṭattum kūṭṭuka.
13. kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭpiṉum - aṅṅaṉan talivaṉiṭattu nikaḻuṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷi veḷippaṭiyākak kāṭṭiya pāṅkaṉ, talivaṉatu āṟṟāmi mikuti kaṇṭu ataṉi nīkkutaṟku virumpuvāṉ. vāyil - pāṅkaṉ. peṭpu viruppam.
kāṭṭu:
9.   “	oṭuṅkī rōti oṇṇutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ
naṟuntaṇ ṇīraḷ āraṇaṅ kiṉaḷē,
iṉiya ḷeṉṟavaṭ puṉiyaḷa vaṟiyēṉ;
cilamel liyavē kiḷavi
aṇimel liyalyāṉ muyaṅkuṅ kālē.”	              (kuṟun - 70)
itu, peṟṟavaḻi makiḻcci.
10. 	kuṇakaṭaṟ ṟiriyatu paṟitapu nāri
tiṇṭērp poṟiyaṉ toṇṭi muṉṟuṟi
ayiri yāririk kaṇavan tāṅkuc
cēya ḷariyōṭ paṭarti
nōyi neñcē nōyppā lōyē. 	           (kuṟun - 128)
itu, pirintavaḻik kalaṅkal.
11. “ tērōṉ ṟeṟukatir maḻuṅkiṉun tiṅkaḷ
tīrā vemmiyoṭu ticinaṭuk kuṟuppiṉum
peyarāp peṟṟiyiṟ ṟiriyāc cīrcāl
kulattiṟ ṟiriyāk koḷkiyuṅ koḷkiyoṭu
nalattiṟ ṟiriyā nāṭṭamum uṭiyōy!
kaṇṭata ṉaḷaviyiṟ kalaṅkuti yeṉiṉim
maṇṭiṇi kiṭakki mānilam
uṇṭeṉak karuti yuṇaralaṉ yāṉē.”
itu, niṟpavi niṉii urittal.
12. 	“nayaṉum naṇpum nāṇunaṉ kuṭimiyum
  payaṉum paṇpum pāṭaṟin toḻukalum
  nummiṉu muṭiyēṉ maṉṉē!. . . . 
  mutunī rilañci pūtta kuvaḷi
  etirmalarp piṇiya laṉṉavivaḷ
 arimatar maḻikkaṇ kāṇā vūṅkē.” 		(naṟ - 160)
itu, nikaḻpavi urittal. kāṇāvūṅku nummiṉum uṭiyēṉ eṉka.
13. “ paṅkayamō tuṅkap paṉitaṅku mālvariyō
aṅkaṇ vicumpō alikaṭalō - eṅkōvic
cevvaṇṇa mālvariyē pōlun tirumēṉi
ivvaṇṇañ ceytārk kiṭam.”
itu, kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭṭal. ‘ivvaṇṇam ceytārkku iṭam eṅkē’ eṉa uṭaṉ paṭṭami kāṇka. itu, iṭamum taṉmiyum viṉāyatu. itaṉpiṉ nikaḻum tuṟikaḷ pala aṭippaṭiyaṉavāy muṉṉiruntaṉa vākiyāl, avaṟṟi uḷḷaṭakkip ‘peṭpiṉum’ eṉṟār.
avvakiyāvaṉa:
1. pāṅkaṉ: 	1. iṭam viṉātal, 2. taṉmi viṉātal.
2. talivaṉ: 	3. iṭaṅkūṟal, 		4. taṉmi kūṟal,
				5. yāṇṭuc ceṉṟu kāṇeṉal.
3. pāṅkaṉ: 	6. kuṟivaḻic cēṟal 	   7. taliviyik kāṇṭal
	8. pāṅkaṉ aiyuṟal 		9. taliviyi viyattal,
	10. talivaṉi viyattal, 11. talivaṟku varuttan 			     takumeṉa viyattal
4. talivaṉ: 	 12. kaṇṭāṉō veṉal.
itaṉāl, paḻantamiḻar kātal vāḻkkaki evvāṟu pōṟṟap paṭṭu vantateṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō?
kāṭṭu:
1, 2 kūṟṟukku muṉ 13kkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa eṭuttuk kāṭṭē.
3.   “	atavat tīṅkaṉi yaṉṉa cemmukat
tuyttali manti vaṉpaṟaḻ tūṅkak
kaḻikka ṇirumpoṟi yēṟi vicitteḻuntu
kuṟakkuṟu mākkaḷ tāḷaṅ koṭṭumak
kuṉṟakat tatuvē kuṟumiḷic cīṟūr,
cīṟū rāḷē nāṟumayirk koṭicci,
koṭicciki yakattatu vēpiṟar
viṭuttaṟ kākātu piṇittaveṉ neñcē.”		   (naṟ - 96)
itu, talivaṉ iṭaṅ kūṟiyatu.
4.   “	kēḷir vāḻiyō kēḷir nāḷumeṉ
neñcupiṇik koṇṭa añci lōti
perunā ṇaṇinta ciṟumel lākam
orunāṭ puṇarap puṇariṉ
arināḷ vāḻkkiyum vēṇṭaleṉ yāṉē”      (kuṟun - 280)
itu, taṉmi kūṟiyatu.
5.   “	kāyā vīṉṟa kaṇavī nāṟṟam
māyā muṉṟil varuvaḷi tūkkum
ākōḷ vāḻnar ciṟukuṭi yāṭṭi
vēyēr meṉṟōḷ vilakkunar
yārō vāḻinī avvayiṟ celiṉē.”
itu, pāṅkaṉi nī yāṇṭuc ceṉṟu kāṇeṉṟatu.
6, 7, 8 pētuṟṟu nīṅkip piriyāt tuyaramoṭu
pōtuṟṟa vēntal pulaṅkoṇṭu - tītuṟṟa
timmayilē pōlu miṉattiṟ pirintutaṉit
timmiyilē ceyta tiṭar.
itu, kuṟivaḻic ceṉṟa pāṅkaṉ taliviyik kaṇṭu, aiyuṟṟuk kūṟiyatu.
9.   “	kaṇpoṟiyaṉ veṟpiṟ kamaḻnīlam meṉmuṟuval
viṇporuteṉ ṉaṉkoṟki veṇmuttam - paṇpayilvāy
avvaḷavaṉ kāviriyā yampavaḷam ikkuṟavar
evvaḷavi ṉīṉṟē meṉal.” 			(kuḻanti)
itu, pāṅkaṉ taliviyi viyantatu.
10. “	paṇṇātu paṇmēṟṟēṉ pāṭuṅ kaḻikkāṉal
eṇṇātu kaṇṭāruk kēraṇaṅkām - eṇṇātu
cāvārcāṉ ṟāṇmi tirintilār maṟṟivaḷik
kāvār kayiṟurī iviṭ ṭār.” 		(tiṇi - nūṟ - 47)
itu, pāṅkaṉ talivaṉi viyantatu.
11. “miṉṉiṉṟa cāyal mikupaṇittōḷ īṅkivaḷmuṉ
eṉṉeṉṟu niṟpā revar.” 			(kuḻanti)
itu, talivaṟku varuttam takumeṉa avaṉi viyantatu.
12. “	kaṇṭaṉaṉ kollō kamaḻtē ṉiruṅkūntal
toṇṭik kaṉivāyt tuṭiyiṭiyit taṇṭaḻipūt
tāṅkiyē niṉṟa taruviṉkaṭ ṭaṉṉāyam
nīṅkiyē niṉṟa nili.” 				(kuḻanti)
itu, pāṅkaṉ taliviyik kaṇṭāṉō veṉṟatu.
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam - 2
itu, kuṟivaḻic ceṉṟa pāṅkaṉ taliviyik kaṇṭu mīṇṭu talivaṉik kūṭutalum, talivaṉ ceṉṟu taliviyik kūṭulumām.
1. pāṅkaṉ talivi taṉmi kūṟal
2. iṭaṅkāṭṭal
3. talivaṉ, taliviyit tōḻiyoṭu varukeṉal
4. pāṅkaṉ vāḻttal
kāṭṭu:
1, 2. 	nañcuṅ kolivēlum naccuvāyk kūrvāḷum
eñcum paṭika ḷelāntāṅki - añcavē
taṉṉi nikarttut taṉiyāka niṟkiṉṟa
tuṉṉi varuttiya voṉṟu
itu, pāṅkaṉ talivaṟkut talivi taṉmi kūṟi iṭaṅ kāṭṭiyatu.
	“emakkunayan taruḷiṉi yāyiṟ paṇittōḷ
	oṇṇuta lariviyoṭu meṉmela viyali
	vanticiṉ vāḻiyō maṭanti
	toṇṭi yaṉṉaniṉ paṇpupala koṇṭē.” 	(aiṅ - 175) - (18)
1. talivi nāṇum maṭaṉum
137. 	kāmat tiṇiyiṟ kaṇṇiṉṟu varūum
nāṇu maṭaṉum peṇmiya vātaliṉ
kuṟippiṉu miṭattiṉum allatu vēṭki
neṟippaṭa vārā avaḷvayi ṉāṉa.
itu, talivi nāṇu maṭaṉu nikaḻak kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvaḷ eṉa, ‘accamum nāṇum’ (kaḷa - 10) eṉpataṟkup puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
i - ḷ: avaḷ vayiṉ āṉa nāṇum maṭaṉum peṇ miyavātaliṉ - taliviyiṭat tuḷavākiya nāṇum maṭaṉum peṇmip paruvattē tōṉṟutali yuṭiyavākaliṉ, kāmattiṇiyiṉ kaṇ niṉṟu kuṟippiṉum varum - appeṇmip paruvattē tōṉṟiya kāmaṅ kāraṇamāka kaṇṇiṉkaṭ kuṟippāka nikaḻum, vēṭki neṟippaṭa iṭattiṉum varum - kātal vaḻippaṭutalālē karuma nikaḻccik kaṇṇum nikaḻum, allatu vārā - avvīriṭattum allatu nāṇum maṭaṉum nikaḻā eṉṟavāṟu.
kāmattiṇi - kaḷavoḻukkam. ‘varum’ eṉpataṉi iraṇṭi ṭattum kūṭṭuka. iṭam - karuma nikaḻcci (cey - 198) atāvatu, iṭantalippāṭum, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamum, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭamum, iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ nikaḻnta ‘pukumukam purital’ mutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭu meyppāṭṭiṉum nāṇu maṭaṉum kuṟippāka nikaḻntavāṟu kāṇka. iyaṟkip puṇarcci, karuma nikaḻcci ākiya ivviraṇṭu mallāta iṭattut talivi nāṇu maṭaṉu nīṅkak kūṟṟu nikaḻttuva ḷeṉpatām. 	(19)
2. nāṇu maṭaṉum taṉmi tirital
138. 	kāmañ collā nāṭṭa miṉmiyiṉ
ēmuṟa viraṇṭum uḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu, karuma nikaḻccikkaṇ nāṇu maṭaṉum taṉmi tirintu varumeṉkiṉṟatu. mēlataṟkōr ciṟappu viti.
i - ḷ : collāk kāmam iṉmiyiṉ - karuma nikaḻcci yiṭattuk kūṟṟu nikaḻāta kāmam pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṉ kaṇ iṉmiyāl, iraṇṭum ēmuṟa nāṭṭam uḷaveṉa moḻipa - muṟkūṟiya nāṇu maṭaṉum tantaṉmi tirintu vara nāṭṭutal uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉṟavāṟu. ēmuṟutal - tirital.
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattup perumpālum nāṇu maṭaṉuṅkeṭat talivi kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvaḷeṉpatām. eṉavē,iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉkaṇ nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkā veṉpatu peṟṟām. tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭak kuṟiyiṭattiliruntē talivi kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvāḷ.
kāṭṭu
pērēmuṟ ṟāypōla muṉṉiṉṟu vilakkumāl
yārellā niṉṉi yaṟinta tūu milvaḻi (kali. 113)
itu, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattut talivi nāṇ maṭaṉīṅkak kūṟiyatu. 	(20)
3. talivi nāṇmaṭaṉ nīṅkak kūṟutal
139. 	colletir moḻital arumit tākaliṉ
alla kūṟṟumoḻi yavaḷvayi ṉāṉa.
i - ḷ : etir col - nāṇu maṭaṉu nīṅkiya colli, avaḷ vayiṉ moḻital arumittu alla ākaliṉ - tōḻiyiṭattuk kūṟutal talivikku arumiyuṭittu allavākiyiṉālē, kūṟṟumoḻi āṉa - kuṟippāṉaṉṟik kūṟṟāṟ kūṟumoḻi talivikkup poruntiṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
etirtal - taṉmi māṟupaṭutal. nāṇmaṭaṉ uḷḷata ṉālēyē talivi kūṟṟāṉ moḻiyātu kuṟippāl moḻiya lāyiṉaḷ. tōḻi taliviyiṉ uṭal pōṉṟavaḷākiyāl avaḷiṭam maṟittuk kūṟavēṇṭuva toṉṟiṉṟu. ākavē, tōḻi yiṭan talivi nāṇmaṭaṉ nīṅka veḷippaṭak kūṟuvaḷeṉpatām.
im mūṉṟu nūṟpākkaḷālum talivi kūṟṟuk kilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappaṭṭatu. talivi nāṇu maṭaṉumuḷḷa peṇmip paruvattā ḷākiyāl, iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ meyppāṭṭuvaḻi kuṟippākat taṉatu karutti veḷiyiṭuvāḷ; annāṇu maṭaṉum iyaṟkip puṇarcciyē yaṉṟi, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉ kaṇṇum nikaḻum (cū - 14); āṉāl, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉ kaṇ perumpālum nāṇumaṭaṉu nīṅkak kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvāḷ (cū - 15); tōḻiyiṭattu nāṇ maṭaṉiṉṟu veḷip paṭiyākavē kūṟuvāḷ (cū - 16) eṉpatām.
kāṭṭu :
	“kūṭutal vēṭkiyāṟ kuṟipārttu 			(kali - 46)
	 vaḷimuṉki paṟṟi naliyat terumantiṭṭu” 	(kali - 51)
ivi, talivi tōḻiyiṭam nāṇu maṭaṉum kūṟiyatu. itu kuṟiyiṭam.
5. kaḷavil talivikkuriyatu
141. 	munnā ḷallatu tuṇiyiṉṟu kaḻiyā
tannā ḷakattum atuvari viṉṟē.
i - ḷ: munnā ḷallatu tuṇi iṉṟu kaḻiyātu - pūppeytiya mūṉṟu nāḷu mallatu talivi kūṭṭamiṉṟi irā. . an nāḷatu varivu aṉṟē - am mūṉṟu nāḷiṉ akappaṭṭa nāḷākiya ōriru  nāḷiṉum tuṇiyiṉṟik kaḻital nīkkap paṭātu.
atāvatu, mātappūp peytiya nāḷ toṭaṅkip pūppu nikaḻum nāḷākiya mūṉṟu nāḷum talivi talivaṉik kūṭappeṟāḷ. ‘annāḷ akattum’ eṉṟatu, pūppup puṟappaṭṭa munnāḷiṉ piṉ oru nāḷum irunāḷum eṉṟavāṟu. mūṉṟu nāḷil pūppu nillātu   mēlum orunāḷ, irunāḷ pūppu variṉ an nāṭkaḷilum talivi talivaṉik kūṭātiruttal eṉpatām.
akappaṭutal - uṭpaṭutal. mūṉṟu nāḷiṉ uṭpaṭṭatu oṉṟum iraṇṭum eṉka. atu - tuṇiyiṉṟik kaḻital. immūṉṟum aintumallāta oru tiṅkaḷil irupattēḻum, irupattintu nāḷum kūṭuvaḷ eṉpatām. iṉi, alla kuṟippaṭṭuḻi orunāḷum irunāḷum tuṇiyiṉṟik kaḻiyap peṟumeṉṟuṇarka.	(23)
6. pāṅkaṉ nimittam
142.	pāṅkaṉ nimittam paṉṉiraṇ ṭeṉpa.
i - ḷ: pāṅkaṉ nimittam paṉṉiraṇṭu vakippaṭum eṉṟavāṟu. nimittam - kāraṇam.
paṉṉiraṇṭāvaṉa: kikkiḷik kuṟippu mūṉṟum (akat - 36), aintiṇiyum (akat - 1) peruntiṇik kuṟippu nāṉkum (akat - 37) ām.
muṉ (kaḷa - 11) puṇarccikku maṭṭum pāṅkaṉimittaṅ kūṟiṉār. īṇṭu mutal nūṟpāvil (akat - 1) kikkiḷi mutalāp peruntiṇiyiṟuvāyk kūṟiya yāvaṟṟiṟkum - paṉṉiraṇṭaṟkum - pāṅkaṉ nimittamāvāṉ eṉpatu kūṟiṉār.	(24) 
7. kikkiḷi peruntiṇi
143.	muṉṉiya mūṉṟuṅ kikkiḷik kuṟippē
piṉṉar nāṉkum peruntiṇi peṟumē.
i - ḷ: muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟum kikkiḷik kuṟippākum; piṟkūṟiya nāṉkum peruntiṇik kuṟippākum.
akattiṇiyiyal 36, 37 nūṟpāvuri pārkka. muṉ iṭantalip pāṭṭiṉ piṉṉart taliviyik kūṭṭutaṟkup pāṅkaṉ nimittam āyiṉāṟpōla, im mūvakik kikkiḷik kuṟippiṉum takāteṉa vilakkalum, ceṉṟu taliviyik kaṇṭu vantu, iḷiyaḷeṉavum, kātaṟ kuṟipput tōṉṟā
tavaḷeṉavum, avvāṟu ceytal muṟiyaṉ ṟeṉavuṅ kūṟi vēṟupaṭuttaluñ ceyvāṉākiyāl pāṅkaṉ nimittamāyiṉāṉ.
avvāṟē, peruntiṇik kuṟippākiya maṭalēṟutal mutaliya nāṉkiṉum pāṅkaṉ iṭiniṉṟu nalvaḻip paṭuppāṉākaliṉ nimittamāyiṉāṉ. puṇarccikkē yaṉṟip pirital mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum pāṅkaṉ nimitta mātali aṟika.	(25)
8. ulakiyaṟ puṇarcci
144.	mutaloṭu puṇarnta yāḻōr mēṉa
tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉ ainnilam peṟumē.
itu, kuṟiñcikkaṇṇē yaṉṟi aivaki nilattiṉum puṇarcci nikaḻu meṉkiṉṟatu. eṉavē, itu akat - 5, 16 cūttiraṅkaṭkuc ciṟappu viti kūṟuvatākum.
i -ḷ: mutaloṭu puṇarnta -  nilamum kālamumākiya mutaṟ poruḷkaḷōṭu kūṭiya, yāḻōr mēṉa - kaḷavoḻukka
māṉatu, taval aruñ ciṟappiṉ - keṭalaruñ ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya, ainnilam peṟumē - kuṟiñci mutaliya aintu nilattiṉ kaṇṇum nikaḻum eṉṟavāṟu.
kaḷavoḻukkam - puṇarcci. taval -keṭal. yāḻōr - tamiḻiciyil valla ōr kuṟiñci vakuppār; kuṟiñci yoḻukkat
tiṟkuc ciṟappuṭiyavar. kaḷa - 2. uri pārkka.
muṉṉark (akat - 16) kuṟiñci nilattiṉkaṇṇē puṇarcci nikaḻumeṉṟār. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṉkaṇ ātalāl, maruta mutaliya maṟṟi nilaṅkaḷiṉum puṇarcci nikaḻumeṉa aiyam nīkkiṉār. vēṉiṟ kālattil mulliyum kuṟiñciyum vaḷam pirintākiya pāli nilattāriṭiyum puṇarcci nikaḻum eṉka. aṅku (akat - 4) mutaṟporuḷ paṟṟi nilatti nālāka vakuttaṉar. itu ulakiyal kūṟiyatu.	(26)


9. tāmē tūtuvarātal
145.	kāmak kūṭṭan taṉimiyiṟ politaliṟ
ṟāmē tūtuva rātalu murittē.
i - ḷ: kāmak kūṭṭam taṉimiyil politaliṉ - iyaṟkip puṇarcciyum iṭantalippāṭum iṭi niṉṟu kūṭṭuvāriṉṟit taṉimiyāl ciṟattaliṉ, tāmē tūtuvar ātalum urittē - talivaṉum taliviyum oruvarkkoruvar tūtuvarāki oruvari yoruvar kūṭutalum āṇṭu urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
atāvatu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamum tōḻiyaṟ kūṭṭamum nikaḻāmal, iyaṟkip puṇarcciyum iṭantalip pāṭum nikaḻntapiṉ varintu koḷḷutalum cilariṭam uṇṭu eṉpatām. iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉum iṭantalip pāṭṭiṉum iṭi niṉṟu kūṭṭuvāriṉṟit talivaṉum taliviyum tāmē oruvari oruvar kātalittuk kūṭutal kāṇka. taṉimiyil polital - tāmākavē kūṭiyiṉpuṟal.	(27)
4. tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam - tōḻiyāl talivaṉum taliviyum kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam. atu,
1. pāṅki matiyuṭampāṭu	5. aṟattoṭu nili
2. kuṟiyiṭam	6. varital
3. varital vēṭki	7. uṭaṉpōkku
4. varivu kaṭātal
eṉa eḻuvakip paṭum.
1. pāṅki matiyuṭampāṭu
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉum, iṭantalip pāṭṭiṉum, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṉum taliviyik kūṭiya talivaṉ, iṉi eṉṟum varuttamiṉṟik kūṭak karutiyavaṉāy, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattē puṇarnta taliviyi āyattuyttu maṟintu niṉṟu kaṇṭa pōtu, taliviyiṉ meyttōḻi iṉṉāḷeṉṟu aṟintu avaḷ uṭampāṭu peṟa eṇṇiṉāṉ. avvāṟē maṟunāḷ tōḻi taṉittirukkum pōtēṉum, taliviyōṭuṭaṉirukkum pōtēṉum ceṉṟu, cila kuṟippu moḻikaḷ kūṟit tōḻiyiṉ karutti yaṟivāṉ. talivaṉatu ak kuṟippu moḻiyiyum ceyaliyuṅ kaṇṭa tōḻi  iruvar karuttiṉiyum tammatiyōṭu oṉṟu paṭuttu ōrntu uṇarvāḷ. ivvāṟu mūvar matiyiṉiyum oṉṟu paṭuttu uṇartal paṟṟi itu, matiyuṭampāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. matiyuṭampaṭuttal eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. itu perumpālum pāṅkiyāl iruvar karuttiyum aṟintu kūṭṭappaṭutalāṉ ‘pāṅki matiyuṭampāṭu’ eṉappaṭṭatu. uṭampāṭu - uṭampaṭutal, oṉṟupaṭutal. mati - aṟivu, karuttu. ittōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattē talivaṉ talivi iruvarukkum uḷḷa urimippāṭu evvaḷavō avvaḷavu  urimi tōḻikkum uṇṭu.
1. mutaṟ kūṭṭam
talivaṉ tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟal
1. talivaṉ
11: 	14. peṭṭa vāyil peṟṟu iravu valiyuṟuttal
	15. ūrviṉātal
	16. pēr viṉātal
	17. keṭuti viṉātal
14. peṭṭa vāyil peṟṟu iravu valiyuṟuttalum - talivikku viruppa muḷḷa tōḻiyit taṉakku vāyilākap peṟṟu, avaḷi ‘irantu piṉṉiṟṟal’ eṉa muṭivu kaṭṭutalum; irantu piṉṉiṟṟal - eṉatu kuṟiyi muṭittut taruvāyeṉak kēṭṭu niṟṟal. kuṟi - taliviyi eppōtum iṭiyīṭiṉṟik kūṭa eṇṇutal. peṭpu - viruppam. vāyil - tuṇi. peṭṭa vāyil - viruppamuḷḷa vāyil. vāyil - tōḻi.
15, 16, 17. ūrum pērum keṭutiyum piṟavum nīriṟ kuṟippiṉ nirampak kūṟit tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟum pakutiyum - tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṟkat tuṇinta talivaṉ, tōḻi taṉittō, taliviyuṭaṉō irukkumpōtu ceṉṟu, num ūrum pērum yāvi yeṉavum, yāṉ keṭuttavi kāṭṭumiṉ eṉavum, aṉiyaṉa piṟavaṟṟiyum aṉpu tōṉṟak kūṟum ikkūṟṟu  vēṟōr karuttu uṭitteṉa avaḷ karutumāṟṟāṉ amiyac collit tōḻikkut taṉ kuṟiyi aṟivikkum kūṟupāṭum.
nīrmi - taṉmi, aṉput taṉmi. nīril kuṟippu - avvaṉpu moḻiyāl vēṟōr karuttut tōṉṟutal.
keṭuti viṉātalāvatu - eṉatu ampu tappi vanta yāṉi, māṉ ivaṟṟik kaṇṭīrō eṉa viṉāvutal. keṭuti - eṉṉāl keṭuti ceyyappaṭṭatu, ampeyyappaṭṭatu.
piṟa - vaḻiviṉātal, avar toḻil viṉātal mutaliyaṉa. yāṉ iṅku taṅkiṉum, eṉṉuṭaṉ pēciṉum varum kēṭeṉ eṉṟalumām.
kāṭṭu
14. “	nītara vanta niṟiyaḻi tuyaraniṉ
āṭukoṭi maruṅkul aruḷi ṉallatu
piṟitiṟ ṟīrā teṉpatu piṉṉiṉ
ṟaṟiyak kūṟukam eḻumō neñcē,
koṟkiyam peruntuṟi kuṉitiri toṭutta 
viḷaṅkumut tuṟikkum veṇpal
paṉmāṇ cāyal paratavar makaṭkē.”
itu, iravu valiyuṟṟatu. ‘makaṭku aṟiyak kūṟukam eḻumō neñcē’ eṉal kāṇka.
15. “	kalluṟṟa nōyvaruttak kālum naṭiyaṟṟēṉ
elluṟ ṟiyāṉum varuntiṉēṉ - villuṟṟa
pūṅka ṇimikkum puruva matimukattīr!
īṅkituvō nummuṭiya vūr?”
itu, ūr viṉāyatu.
16. “	ceṟikura lēṉaṟ ciṟukiḷi kāppīr!
aṟikuvē ṉummi viṉāay - aṟipaṟavi
aṉṉa nikarkkuñcīr āṭami meṉṟōḷir
eṉṉa peyarirō nīr?”
itu, peyar viṉāyatu.
17. “	taṇṭu purikatirt tāḻkuraṟ centiṉi
maṇṭupu kavarum māṇṭakiḷi māṟṟum 
oṇṭoṭip paṇittō ḷoṇṇuta liḷiyīr!
kaṇṭaṉa rāyiṟ karavā turimiṉ
koṇṭaṉa kuḻuvi ṉīṅki maṇṭiya
uḷḷaḻi pakaḻiyō ṭuyaṅkiyōr
puḷḷi māṉkali pōkiya neṟiyē?”
itu, keṭuti viṉāyatu.
      “	mellilip parappiṉ viruntuṇ ṭiyāṉumik
kalleṉ ciṟukuṭit taṅkiṉmaṟ ṟevaṉō?”	            (akam - 110)
itu, yāṉ iṅku taṅkip pōkalāmō eṉṟatu.
      “	illuṭik kiḻami emmoṭu puṇariṉ
tītu muṇṭō māta rīrē.” 			            (akam - 290)
itu, eṉṉōṭu pēciṉāl ākātō eṉṟatu.
ivvāṟu aṅku cellumpōtu talivaṉ kaṇṇi, tār, taḻi mutaliya kiyuṟi koṇṭu celvāṉ; akkiyuṟiyit talivikkuk koṭukkumpaṭi tōḻiyiṭam koṭuppāṉ. tōḻi atit taliviyiṭam koṭuttu ēṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi colvāḷ. talivi akkāṇikkiyi ēṟṟuk koḷvatē tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭamākum. iṉṟum taṉ kātalikkuc ciṟṟuṇṭi, tuṇi, pū mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu cellutal kāṇka. kaṇṇi - muṭiyiṟ kaṭṭum pū. tār - māli. taḻi - taḻiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa uṭi. itu, taḻi eṉavē vaḻaṅkum.
taḻiyuṭiyāvatu - palavakiyāṉa iḷantaḻik kottuk
kaḷiyum pūṅkottukkaḷiyuṅ kalantu, māvilit tōraṇam pōlak koṭiyil vittuk kaṭṭi, ōratti oḻuṅkākak kattarittu uṭikku mēl eṭuttuk koḷḷum uṭi. itu viḷiyāṭumpōtu uṭuppatu, itu kuṟiñci nila iḷam peṇṭir virumpi uṭukkum uṭiyākum. aḻakāṉa taḻiyuṭi kaṭṭik koṭuttut taṉ kātaliyi makiḻvippatu kātalaṉ kaṭamiyākum.
kāṭṭu
       “	muṭitta kulli iliyuṭi naṟumpūc
ceṅkāl marātta vāliṇa riṭiyiṭupu
curumpuṇat toṭutta peruntaṇ māttaḻi
tiruntukā ḻalkul tiḷippa vuṭīi.”	    (muruku - 210 - 4)
pacuntaḻiyum maravuriyum icintiṭavē uṭuppōm.
(mīṉāṭciyammi kuṟam)
eṉak kāṇka.
2. tōḻi ciṟappu
146.  cūḻtalum ucāttuṇi nilimiyiṟ polimē.
i - ḷ : ucāttuṇi nilimiyiṉ - talivaṉum taliviyum ucāvutaṟkut tuṇiyākiya nilimiyiṉālē, cūḻtalum polimē - iruvarkkum puṇarcciyuṇmiyi nāṟṟam mutaliya eḻuvaki yāṉum (kaḷa - 13) ārāytalum tōḻikku urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
cūḻtal - ārāytal. polital - ārāyum pōtu talimi eytutal. ucāvutal - viṉāvutal. atāvatu, talivaṉ taṉatu kuṟi muṭikkum paṭiyum, talivi taṉatu varital vēṭkiyiyum viṉāvutalām.
avvāṟu talivaṉum taliviyum taṅkaṭku vēṇṭiyatik kēṭṭu muṭittuk koḷḷum tuṇiviyāy iruppataṉāl, tōḻi avviruvaratu kūṭṭa muṇmiyi eḻuvakiyāṉ ārāyun talimiyum peṟuvaḷ eṉpatām.
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ talimik kiḻukkāyt tōḻi aṉṉār kaḷavoḻukkatti ārāytal kuṟṟamēṉum, avar tam kaḷavoḻukkattiṟku avaḷiyē tuṇiyākak koṇṭuḷḷatāl, ārāytal poruntumeṉa vaḻuvamittavāṟām. eṭuttuk kāṭṭup piṉṉark kāṭṭappaṭum.	(28)
3. tōḻi ārāycci
147.	kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartal muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal
iruvaru muḷvaḻi avaṉvara vuṇartaleṉa
matiyuṭam paṭuttal orumū vakittē
i - ḷ : 1. kuṟiyuṟa uṇartal - talivaṉ tōḻiyit taṉ kuṟi muṭikkumpaṭi vēṇṭutalāl uṇartal, 2. muṉṉuṟa uṇartal - kuṟippāl mikavum uṇartal, 3. iruvarum uḷ vaḻi avaṉ vara uṇartal - taliviyum tōḻiyum oruṅkirunta vaḻit talivaṉ varutalāl, talivaṉ kuṟippum talivi kuṟippum kaṇṭu uṇartal, eṉa matiyuṭam paṭuttal oru mūvakittē - eṉa iruvar karuttiṉiyum taṉ karuttōṭu oṉṟu paṭuttu uṇartal mūṉṟu vakippaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
muṉ - muṉṉam - kuṟippu, uṟa - mika. mūṉṟāṉum kūṭṭa muṇmiyi uṇartalām. matiyuṭam paṭuttal - iruvar uḷḷak karuttiṉiyum - iruvarum oruvari yoruvar kātalikkiṟār eṉpati ārāyntaṟital.
1. muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal, 2. kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartal, 3. iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉ vara uṇartal eṉa muṟippaṭuttuka.
kāṭṭu
1. “	tōḷā relvaḷi teḷirppa niṉpōl
yāṉu māṭik kāṇkō tōḻi.”
itu, muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal. itil, kūṭṭa muṇarātāḷ pōla, nāṇiṟku māṟākāmal kūṟutal kāṇka.
2. “	niṉṉiṉ viṭāa niḻalpōṟ ṟiritaruvāy
eṉṉī peṟātati teṉ.”				(kali - 61)
itu, kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartal.
3.   “	ēṉal kāval ivaḷu mallaḷ
māṉvaḻi varukuvaṉ ivaṉu mallaṉ
narantaṅ kaṇṇi ivaṉō ṭivaḷiṭik
karanta vuḷḷamoṭu karutiyatu piṟitē.”
itu, avaṉvara vuṇartal.	(29)
4. eṇvaki ārāycci
tōḻi matiyuṭampaṭuṅkāl avaḷ ārāyum ārāycci yellām nāṭṭam eṉappaṭum. atu eṭṭām. avaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉuṟu puṇarcciyi uṇartaṟkuriyavi nāṟṟam mutaliya ēḻām. muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci - muṉṉamē nikaḻnta puṇarcci.
13. 	1. nāṟṟam	5. ceyviṉi maṟippu
	2. tōṟṟam	6. celavu
	3. oḻukkam	7. payilvu
	4. uṇṭi	8. palvēṟu kavar poruḷ nāṭṭam
i - ḷ : nāṟṟam mutaliya ēḻum muṉṉuṟu vuṇartalākum. puṇarcci etirppāṭu - etirppāṭu puṇarcci eṉa māṟuka. etirppāṭu puṇarcciyāvatu - koṭuppāriṉṟi iruvarum tāmē etirppaṭṭup puṇarum puṇarcci. uḷḷuṟuttu varūum uṇarcci ēḻiṉum uṇarnta piṉṟi - ap puṇarcci etirppāṭu nikaḻntataṉit tōḻi taṉ uḷḷattuḷḷē eḻunta aiyavuṇarviṉi, nāṟṟa mutaliya ēḻiṉālum teḷintu puṇarcci uṇṭeṉpati uṇarnta piṉ. ēḻiṉālum uṇarnta piṉpē taliviyiṭam tōḻi collāṭuvāḷ eṉpataṟkup piṉṟi eṉṟār. allatu, uṇarntapiṉ meyyiṉum poyyiṉum nāṭu meṉka. ivvēḻiṉālum muṉpē iruvarum etirppaṭṭup puṇarntuḷḷaṉar eṉṉum muṉṉuṟu puṇarcciyit tōḻi aṟivāḷ.
uḷḷuṟuttu varūum uṇarcci - uḷḷattuḷ eḻum aiyavuṇarvu. uṇarcci ēḻiṉum - uṇarcciyi ēḻiṉālum.
1. nāṟṟamāvatu - talivi kūntalum neṟṟiyum pētip paruvattiṟkuttaka maṇakkāmal, talivaṉ kūṭṭattāl cantaṉam, kuṅkumam, puṉuku, palvakip pūkkaḷ ivi kalantu maṇattal.
2. tōṟṟamāvatu - kaṇ kāmakkuṟippik kāṭṭutal; tōḷum mārpum peruttuk kāṭṭutal.
3. oḻukkamāvatu - viḷiyāṭṭu makaḷiroṭukūṭi, muṟṟilāl maṇal koḻittuc cōṟākkutal mutaliyavaṟṟi veṟutta kuṟippiṉaḷāyp peṇṭaṉmik kēṟpa oḻukutal. muṟṟil - ciṟumuṟam.
4. uṇṭiyāvatu - muṉ pāl mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu ūṭṭa uṇṭu vantavaḷ, ippōtu oḻukkamum nāṇamum kāmamum mikavē, ati veṟutta uḷḷattāḷātal.
